Henry Kanywamusaayi 58, eyali omukungu wa kkampuni y'essimu eya UTL attiddwa mu bukambwe ne mutabani we Emma Kanywamusaayi ow'emyaka 20 ne baleka abalala babiri nga bapooca n'ebiwundu.
Bano baasangiddwa mu maka gaabwe e Lweza B mu Makindye Ssaabagabo.
Abatemu okutta abantu bano, baalinnye ekikomera ky'awaka ne bagwa munda ne baggula enzigi ku ssaawa nga 8:00 ez'ekiro ekyakeesezza Olwokubiri.
Abatemu baatuukidde mu kisenge omwabadde taata n'ekya mutabani we ne batandika okubatema.
Nnamwandu Jane Kanywamusaayi, yategeezezza nti yabadde mu tulo mu kisenge kya wansi nawulira nga mutabani we Emma Kanywamusaayi alaajana.
Amangu ago yagenze okuwulira nga waliwo okulwanagana okw'amaanyi.
Nnamwandu agamba nti bakira ababbi basaba buli omu ssente nga kwe batadde okubatema ebijambiya, okubakuba empi n'ensambaggere nga bababuuza lwaki temuli ssente mu nnyumba.
Olwavudde eyo, abatemu baalumbye ekisenge omwabadde muwala we Dorah Kanywamusaayi ne bamukuba bubi oluvannyuma ne bamutuukako naye ne bamuyisaamu empi n'ensambaggere nga bwe babaggyako ssente zonna ne bwe ziba za binusu.
Oluvannyuma baatutte amasimu gonna, ne ttivvi. Ebikwata ku Kanywamusaayi Kyategeerekese nti Kanywamusaayi yali yadduka awaka oluvannyuma lw'okufuna obutakkaanya ne mukyala we n'agenda ew'omukyala omulala e Ntebe gy'abadde abeera.
Wabula wiiki nga emu emabega, abadde yakomawo awaka nga kiteeberezebwa okuba nga yali afunye obutakkaanya ne mukyala muto gy'abadde abeera.
Abamu ku batuuze bategeezezza nti engeri Kanywamusaayi gye yagendanga ebweru, abatemu kyandiba nga baalowoozezza nti ebbanga eryo ly'abadde taliiwo abadde bweru era nga basuubira nti yabadde akomyewo ne ssente.
POLIISI ky'etuuseeko
Poliisi e Kajjansi yaleese embwa ezikonga olusu ne zisibira mu muzigo ogubadde gupangisibwa abavubuka bana abatuuze be bamanyiiko nga Wasswa ne Kato n'abalala poliisi n'ebakwata n'ebatwala ku poliisi okubayambako okubuuliriza.
Omwogezi wa Poliisi mu Kampala n'emiriraano Luke Owoyesigyire ategeezezza nti abatemu abasse omwana ne kitaawe, tebaabadde ba bijambiya wabula kiteeberezebwa okuba nga baabakubye butayimbwa na miggo.
Akakasizza okukwatibwa kw'abantu bana n'atayatuukiriza mannya olw'obutataataaganya kunoonyereza.

Gwe ssebo olwo olususu lulya mukene? Nze nno sirina ky’amaanyi kye ndya okuggyako ettooke n’ennyama y’embuzi. Mpozzi ng’attako Yogurt. Naye endabika eraga nti bakuzaala Mbarara. Nedda ssebo, siri weeyo naye ngwayo mpolampola kubanga nninayo abantu bange.
Kyana ggwe Kemi ng’olabika bulungi. Ani akussaamu ssente?
Tewali kye nkozesa wadde anteekamu ssente. Bwentyo bwe nazaalibwa. Ndi ku origino.
Ekitegeeza omwami wo akuwa emirembe n’olabika bulungi?
Eby’abasajja tetubigendamu kubanga simulina ate sinoonya. Naye waliwo alyawo ku kabalaza agezaako okuntaayiza.
Mpozzi gwe ani amannya?
Ndi muyimbi nga nze nayimba ennyimba nga Akagatto, Omponyezza, Omukwano lugoye, ne Malala. Wabula akulabirira mu byokulya alina kwesiba bbiri.
Nze nno sirina ky’amaanyi kye ndya okuggyako ettooke n’ennyama y’embuzi. Mpozzi ng’attako Yogurt. Naye endabika eraga nti bakuzaala Mbarara. Nedda ssebo, siri weeyo naye ngwayo mpolampola kubanga nninayo abantu bange.
Kuyimba nnyimba zange abantu ne bazaagala. Okuziwulira ku leediyo ne ttivvi ne mu mawulire nga banjogerako bimpa emirembe.

Nze simanyi kulimba.

Enzirugavu kubanga eggyayo langi yange ne njaka.

‘Abateebi banjiyeeyo mu maaso ga Fergie’
Abapoliisi abalabikidde mu katambi nga batulugunya owa 'people...
Abazannyi ba Villa nga baakamala okutendekebwa eggulo ku Villa Park. Wansi ye mutendesi waabwe Kirya.
Lwakutaano mu za Afrika Vipers - Enyimba e Nakivubo Ebisale: 5,000/- ne 10,000/- Lwamukaaga mu za Afrika Al Karoutum - SC Villa e Sudan Nga beetegekera okukyalira El Khartoum Watanyi mu za CAF Confederations Cup, omutendesi wa Villa, Ibrahim Kirya ttiimu ye yagitutte Njeru ku kisaawe ky’ekiwempe.
Kirya agamba nti olw’okuba ng’e Sudan bagenda kuzannyira ku kiwempe, asazeewo ttiimu agitwale e Njeru etendekerweyo. “Olunaku olumu lutumala okumanyiira ekiwempe kubanga mu bbanga eritali ly’ewala twabadde Rwanda era ne tuzannya egy’omukwano egiwerako ku bisaawe eby’ekiwempe.
Nkakasa abazannyi bange balina obumanyirivu ku kiwempe ate nakyo si kirungi kuzannyirako kumala bbanga ddene kubanga kibaviirako okufuna obuvune,” Kirya bwe yategeezezza oluvannyuma lw’okutendekebwa okw’oku makya eggulo ku Villa Park e Nsambya.
Okusinziira ku Frank ‘Video’ Anyau, akulira ebyekikugu mu SC Villa, baabadde n’entegeka okuzannyamu ogw’omukwano n’emu ku ttiimu ennene leero kiwe abazannyi baabwe okugezesebwa okusembayo.
Ttiimu esitula Lwakuna okwolekera Sudan mu nnyonyi ya Ethiopian Airlines. Villa, yasemba okukiika mu mpaka za Afrika mu 2004 bwe yaggyibwamu Bakili Bullets eya Malawi ku mugatte gwa ggoolo 2-1 mu gw’oluzannya lw’okusunsulamu. Bakili Bullets yawangulira omwayo bwe bajja e Kampala, ne balemagana (0-0).
Nsimbe agumizza abawagizi: WADDE nga George Nsimbe yaakamala wiiki emu yokka ku butendesi bwa Vipers, aweze nga bw’egenda okuwangula Enyimba ey’e Nigeria agiwandule mu mpaka za CAF Champions League.
Ku Lwokutaano, Vipers ettunka ne Enyimba mu gw’oluzannya olusooka mu kisaawe e Nakivubo, Nsimbe omupiira gw’agamba nti waakuguwangula akakase abawagizi nti ky’akola akimanyi. Vipers tekiikangako mu mpaka za Afrika okuggyako eza CECAFA.

24 N. Mubele for G. Imbula 66'

Ddala Oyinza Okubeera Mukwano gwa Katonda? _ Funa Enkolagana
EMIREMBE N’ESSANYU
OBUFUMBO N’AMAKA
EBIBUUZO BAYIBULI BY’EDDAMU
LAYIBULALE KU MUKUTU GWAFFE
OKUJJUKIRA OKUFA KWA YESU
ESSUULA 1 ‘Laba! Ono ye Katonda waffe’
ESSUULA 2 Ddala Oyinza ‘Okufuna Enkolagana Ennungi ne Katonda’?
EKITUNDU 1 ‘Alina Amaanyi Mangi’
ESSUULA 5 Amaanyi ge Yakozesa Okutonda—Oyo “Eyakola Eggulu n’Ensi”
ESSUULA 7 Amaanyi ag’Obukuumi—‘Katonda Kye Kiddukiro Kyaffe’
ESSUULA 8 Amaanyi ag’Okuzza Obuggya​—Yakuwa ‘Azza Obuggya Ebintu Byonna’
ESSUULA 9 “Kristo Amaanyi ga Katonda”
ESSUULA 10 ‘Mukoppe Katonda’ mu Ngeri Gye Mukozesaamu Amaanyi
ESSUULA 2 “Ayagala Obwenkanya”
ESSUULA 11 “Amakubo Ge Gonna ga Bwenkanya”
ESSUULA 12 “Katonda Si Mwenkanya?”
ESSUULA 13 “Amateeka ga Yakuwa Gaatuukirira”
ESSUULA 14 Yakuwa Akola Enteekateeka ‘y’Ekinunulo ku lw’Abangi’
ESSUULA 15 Yesu ‘Aleeta Obwenkanya mu Nsi’

ESSUULA 21 Yesu Abikkula “Amagezi Agava eri Katonda”

ESSUULA 24 Tewali Kiyinza “Kutwawukanya na Kwagala kwa Katonda”
ESSUULA 25 ‘Obusaasizi bwa Katonda Waffe’
ESSUULA 27 ‘Obulungi Bwe nga Bungi!’
ESSUULA 29 “Okutegeera Okwagala kwa Kristo”
ESSUULA 30 “Mutambulirenga mu Kwagala”
ESSUULA 31 “Funa Enkolagana Ennungi ne Katonda, Naye Anaabeera Mukwano Gwo”
Bino wandyagadde okubisoma mu %%?
Ddala Oyinza ‘Okufuna Enkolagana Ennungi ne Katonda’?
1, 2. (a) Kiki bangi kye bayinza okulowooza nti tekisoboka, naye Baibuli etukakasa ki? (b) Ibulayimu yalina nkolagana ki ne Katonda, era lwaki?
WANDIWULIDDE otya singa Omutonzi w’eggulu n’ensi akwogerako nti, “Ono mukwano gwange”? Bangi bayinza okulowooza nti ekyo tekisoboka. Omuntu obuntu ayinza atya okuba mukwano gwa Yakuwa Katonda? Kyokka, Baibuli etukakasiza ddala nti tusobola okuba n’enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda.
2 Ibulayimu eyaliwo mu biseera ebyayita yalina enkolagana ng’eyo. Omusajja oyo ow’edda ennyo Yakuwa yamwogerako nga ‘mukwano gwe.’ (Isaaya 41:8) Yee, Yakuwa yatwala Ibulayimu okuba mukwano gwe. Ibulayimu yalina enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda olw’okuba ‘yakkiririza mu Katonda.’ (Yakobo 2:23) Leero, Yakuwa ‘asanyukira’ abo abamuweereza olw’okuba bamwagala. (Ekyamateeka 10:15) Ekigambo kye kitukubiriza: “Funa enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda naye anaabeera mukwano gwo.” (Yakobo 4:8, NW) Mu bigambo ebyo tukubirizibwa okufuna enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda era n’atusuubiza nti ajja kubeera mukwano gwaffe.
3. Yakuwa ayagala tukole ki, era kisuubizo ki ekikwataganyizibwa nakyo?
3 Yakuwa ayagala tufune enkolagana ennungi naye. Ayagala tubeere mikwano gye. Mu kiseera kye kimu, atusuubiza nti singa tubaako kye tukola okubeera mikwano gye naye ajja kubeera mukwano gwaffe. Ajja kuba n’enkolagana ennungi naffe. Bwe kityo, tuyinza okufuna ekintu eky’omuwendo ddala—“omukwano ne Yakuwa.” * (Zabbuli 25:14, NW) Wano ekigambo “omukwano” kirina amakulu g’okunyumya eby’ekyama ne mukwano gwo nfiirabulago.
4. Wandinnyonnyodde otya ow’omukwano ow’oku lusegere, era Yakuwa yeeraze atya okuba ow’omukwano ng’oyo eri abo abalina enkolagana ennungi naye?
4 Olina mukwano gwo ow’oku lusegere gw’obuulira ebyama byo? Ow’omukwano ow’engeri eyo aba akufaako. Omuteekamu obwesige kubanga mwesigwa. Weeyongera okusanyuka bw’omutegeeza ebikusanyusa. Osobola okugumiikiriza ennaku gy’olina kubanga awuliriza by’omutegeeza. Wadde nga kiyinza okulabika nti abalala bonna tebakutegeera, ye, aba akutegeera. Mu ngeri y’emu, bw’obeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda, oba ofunye Mukwano gwo nfiirabulago akutwala ng’omuntu ow’omuwendo, akufaako ennyo, era akutegeerera ddala. (Zabbuli 103:14; 1 Peetero 5:7) Omutegeeza enneewulira yo ey’omunda, kubanga okimanyi nti mwesigwa eri abo abamwesiga. (Zabbuli 18:25, NW) Kyokka, osobola okuba n’omukwano ng’ogwo ne Katonda kubanga ye kennyini akisobozesezza.
5. Yakuwa yakola ki okutusobozesa okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi naye?
5 Ku bwaffe, ng’abantu aboonoonyi, tetwandisobodde kufuna nkolagana nnungi ne Katonda. (Zabbuli 5:4) Omutume Pawulo yagamba: “Katonda atenderezesa okwagala kwe ye gye tuli, kubanga bwe twali nga tukyalina ebibi Kristo n’atufiirira.” (Abaruumi 5:8) Yee, Yakuwa yakola enteekateeka Yesu ‘aweeyo obulamu bwe ng’ekinunulo ku lw’abangi.’ (Matayo 20:28) Okukkiriza kwe tulina mu kinunulo ekyo kutusobozesa okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda. Okuva Katonda ‘bwe yasooka okutwagala,’ yatuteerawo omusingi ogutusobozesa okubeera mikwano gye.—1 Yokaana 4:19.
6, 7. (a) Tumanya tutya nti Yakuwa si Katonda eyeekwese, atayinza kumanyibwa? (b) Yakuwa atutegeezezza atya ebimukwatako?
6 Yakuwa alina ekirala ky’akoze: Atutegeezezza ebimukwatako. Omukwano gwonna okusobola okubeera ogw’oku lusegere, kyesigama ku kumanyira ddala omuntu, kwe kugamba, okusiima engeri ze n’amakubo ge. N’olwekyo, singa Yakuwa yali Katonda eyeekwese, atayinza kumanyibwa, tetwandisobodde kubeera na nkolagana nnungi naye. Mu kifo ky’okwekweka, ayagala tumumanye. (Isaaya 45:19) Ate era, ebimukwatako biyinza okumanyibwa buli omu, wadde ffe ensi beetwala okuba aba wansi.—Matayo 11:25.
Yakuwa alaze ky’ali okuyitira mu bye yatonda wamu n’Ekigambo kye
7 Yakuwa atutegeezezza atya ebimukwatako? Emirimu gye egy’obutonzi gyoleka ezimu ku ngeri ze, gamba, amaanyi ge amangi, amagezi ge amangi, n’okwagala kwe okungi. (Abaruumi 1:20) Kyokka, Yakuwa tatutegeeza bimukwatako kuyitira mu bye yatonda mwokka. Buli kiseera Yakuwa atuyigiriza ebimukwatako mu ngeri ey’ekitalo, era kino akikoze ng’ayitira mu Kigambo kye, Baibuli.
Okulaba ‘Obulungi bwa Yakuwa’
8 Baibuli yennyini bujulizi obulaga nti Yakuwa atwagala. Mu Kigambo kye ekyo, atutegeeza ebimukwatako mu ngeri gye tuyinza okutegeera—obukakafu obulaga nti atwagala nnyo era ayagala tumumanye era tumwagale. Bye tusoma mu kitabo kino eky’omuwendo bitusobozesa okulaba ‘obulungi bwa Yakuwa’ era bituleetera okwagala okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi naye. (Zabbuli 90:17) Ka tulabe engeri Yakuwa gy’atutegeezaamu ebimukwatako okuyitira mu Kigambo kye, Baibuli.
9. Byawandiikibwa ki mu Baibuli ebyogera ku zimu ku ngeri za Katonda?
9 Ebyawandiikibwa bingi byogera butereevu ku ngeri za Katonda. Weetegereze ezimu ku zo. “Yakuwa ayagala obwenkanya.” (Zabbuli 37:28, NW) “Yakuwa alina amaanyi mangi.” (Yobu 37:23, NW) ‘Ndi mwesigwa, bw’atyo Yakuwa bw’ayogera.’ (Yeremiya 3:12, NW) ‘Wa magezi mu mutima.’ (Yobu 9:4) Ye “Katonda ajjudde okusaasira era ow’ekisa, alwawo okusunguwala, era alina okusaasira okungi n’amazima amangi.” (Okuva 34:6) “Ggwe, Mukama, oli mulungi, oyanguwa okusonyiwa.” (Zabbuli 86:5) Nga bwe kyayogerwako mu ssuula esooka, alina engeri esinga zonna obukulu: “Katonda kwagala.” (1 Yokaana 4:8) Bw’ofumiitiriza ku ngeri zino ennungi, tosikirizibwa eri Katonda ono atalina amwenkana?
Baibuli etuyamba okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Yakuwa
10, 11. (a) Kiki Yakuwa kye yateeka mu Kigambo kye okutuyamba okutegeera obulungi engeri ze? (b) Kyakulabirako ki eky’omu Baibuli ekituyamba okulaba engeri Katonda gye yakozesaamu amaanyi ge?
10 Ng’oggyeko okututegeeza engeri ze, Yakuwa yateeka mu Kigambo kye ebyokulabirako ebiwerako ebiraga engeri gy’azoolesaamu. Ebyokulabirako ebyo bituyamba okutegeera obulungi engeri ze ez’enjawulo. Ate ekyo ne kitusikiriza gy’ali. Lowooza ku kyokulabirako kino.
11 Bw’osoma nti Katonda ‘alina amaanyi mangi,’ oyinza okukwatibwako mu ngeri emu. (Isaaya 40:26) Kyokka, ojja kukwatibwako nnyo n’okusingawo, bw’osoma ku ngeri gye yanunulamu eggwanga lya Isiraeri okuyita mu Nnyanja Emmyufu era n’alirabirira mu ddungu okumala emyaka 40. Oyinza okukuba ekifaananyi ng’amazzi geeyawulamu. Oyinza okukuba ekifaananyi ng’eggwanga eryo—oboolyawo abantu nga 3,000,000—nga bayita awakalu ku ntobo y’ennyanja, ng’amazzi geesimbye ng’ebisenge eruuyi n’eruuyi. (Okuva 14:21; 15:8) Oyinza okulaba engeri Katonda gye yakuumamu eggwanga eryo mu ddungu. Amazzi gaakulukuta okuva mu njazi. Emmere efaanana ensigo enjeru yalabika ku ttaka. (Okuva 16:31; Okubala 20:11) Wano Yakuwa talaga bulazi nti alina amaanyi naye era nti agakozesa ku lw’abantu be. Tekituzzaamu nnyo amaanyi okumanya nti essaala zaffe zituuka eri Katonda ow’amaanyi, ‘ekiddukiro kyaffe, n’amaanyi gaffe, era omubeezi waffe mu nnaku?’—Zabbuli 46:1.
12 Yakuwa, omuntu ow’omwoyo, akoze n’ekisingawo okutuyamba okumumanya. Ng’abantu, tulaba ebyo byokka ebirabika eri amaaso gaffe, era nga n’olwekyo tetuyinza kulaba bya mwoyo. Katonda okutunnyonnyola ky’ali mu ngeri y’omwoyo kyandibadde ng’okugezaako okunnyonnyola omuntu eyazaalibwa nga muzibe engeri gy’ofaananamu, gamba nga langi y’amaaso go, oba amabala agali ku lususu lwo. Mu kifo ky’ekyo, Yakuwa atuyamba okumutegeera. Emirundi egimu akozesa ebyokulabirako, ne yeegeraageranya ku bintu bye tumanyi. Era yeeyogerako ng’alina engeri z’obuntu. *
13. Isaaya 40:11 luwa kifaananyi ki, era kikukwatako kitya?
13 Weetegereze engeri Yakuwa gy’ayogerwako mu Isaaya 40:11: “Ng’omusumba, alikuŋŋaanya abaana b’endiga mu mukono gwe, n’abasitula mu kifuba kye.” Wano Yakuwa ageraageranyizibwa ku musumba asitula abaana b’endiga mu “mukono gwe.” Kino kyoleka obusobozi bwa Katonda okukuuma n’okuwagira abantu be, wadde abo abayinza okuba mu kabi okusinga abalala. Tuyinza okuwulira obukuumi mu mikono gye egy’amaanyi, kubanga bwe tubeera abeesigwa gy’ali, tagenda kutwabulira. (Abaruumi 8:38, 39) Omusumba Omukulu asitula abaana b’endiga “mu kifuba kye”—ebigambo ebitegeeza engoye omusumba z’aba ayambadde, ng’emirundi egimu mwe yasituliranga omwana gw’endiga ogwakazaalibwa. Bwe kityo, tukakasibwa nti Yakuwa atwagala nnyo era atufaako nnyo. Twandibadde twagala okuba n’enkolagana ennungi naye.
‘Omwana Ayagala Okumututegeeza’
14. Lwaki kiyinza okugambibwa nti Yakuwa atutegeeza ebimukwatako okuyitira mu Yesu?
14 Mu Kigambo kye, Yakuwa atutegeeza ebimukwatako okuyitira mu Mwana we omwagalwa, Yesu. Tewali n’omu yandisobodde kwoleka ndowooza ya Katonda n’enneewulira ye oba okunnyonnyola ky’ali, okusinga Yesu. Ekyo tekyewuunyisa, kubanga Omwana oyo omubereberye yali ne Kitaawe ng’ebitonde ebirala eby’omwoyo wamu n’obwengula nga tebinnaba kutondebwa. (Abakkolosaayi 1:15) Yesu amanyi nnyo Yakuwa. Eyo ye nsonga lwaki yali asobola okugamba: “Tewali muntu amanyi Omwana bw’ali, wabula Kitaffe; newakubadde Kitaffe bw’ali, wabula Omwana, n’oyo Omwana gw’ayagala [okumutegeeza].” (Lukka 10:22) Bwe yali ku nsi ng’omuntu, Yesu yategeeza ebikwata ku Kitaawe mu ngeri bbiri enkulu.
15, 16. Ngeri ki ebbiri Yesu mwe yayitira okututegeeza ebikwata ku Kitaawe?
15 Okusooka, ebyo Yesu bye yayigiriza bituyamba okumanya Kitaawe. Yesu yayogera ku Yakuwa mu ngeri ekwata ku mitima gyaffe. Ng’ekyokulabirako, okusobola okunnyonnyola Katonda ow’ekisa akkiriza aboonoonyi abeenenyezza, Yesu yageraageranya Yakuwa ku taata asonyiwa era akwatibwako ennyo bw’alaba omwana we eyayonoona eby’obusika bwe ng’akomawo, n’atuuka n’okudduka n’amugwa mu kifuba n’amunywegera. (Lukka 15:11-24) Yesu era yalaga Yakuwa okuba Katonda ‘asembeza’ abantu ab’emitima emyesigwa olw’okuba abaagala. (Yokaana 6:44, NW) Amanya n’enkazalugya lw’egwa wansi. Yesu yagamba: “Temutyanga; mmwe musinga enkazaluggya ennyingi.” (Matayo 10:29, 31) Mazima ddala, tusikirizibwa eri Katonda ng’oyo afaayo.
16 Ekyokubiri, ekyokulabirako kya Yesu kennyini kitulaga Yakuwa ky’ali. Yesu yayolekera ddala Kitaawe ky’ali n’atuuka n’okugamba nti: “Alabye ku nze, ng’alabye ku Kitange.” (Yokaana 14:9) Bwe kityo, bwe tusoma ku Yesu mu Njiri—enneewulira gye yayoleka era n’engeri gye yayisaamu abalala—tuba ng’abalaba Kitaawe. Tewali ngeri esinga eyo bulungi Yakuwa mwe yandiyitidde okututegeereza ddala engeri ze. Lwaki?
17. Nnyonnyola Yakuwa ky’akoze okutuyamba okutegeera ky’ali.
17 Okuwaayo ekyokulabirako: Kuba ekifaananyi ng’ogezaako okunnyonnyola ekigambo “ekisa.” Oyinza okukinnyonnyola mu bigambo. Naye singa oba osobola okusonga ku muntu akola ekikolwa eky’ekisa n’ogamba, “Ekikolwa ekyo kye kiraga ekisa,” ekigambo “ekisa” kifuna amakulu agasingawo era kiba kyangu okutegeera. Yakuwa akoze ekintu ekifaananako bwe kityo okutuyamba okutegeera ky’ali. Ng’oggyeko okunnyonnyola ky’ali mu bigambo, atuwadde ekyokulabirako ky’Omwana we. Okuyitira mu Yesu, tusobola okulaba engeri za Katonda. Okuyitira mu Njiri ezoogera ku Yesu, Yakuwa aba atugamba: “Bwe ntyo bwe ndi.” Ebyawandiikibwa ebyaluŋŋamizibwa byogera bitya ku Yesu bwe yali ku nsi?
18. Yesu yayoleka atya amaanyi, obwenkanya n’amagezi?
18 Engeri enkulu ennya eza Katonda zeeyoleka bulungi mu ebyo Yesu bye yakola. Yayoleka amaanyi ku ndwadde, enjala n’okufa. Kyokka, okwawukana ku bantu abeefaako bokka abakozesa obubi amaanyi gaabwe, ye teyakozesaako maanyi ago ag’ekyamagero okweganyula oba okulumya abalala. (Matayo 4:2-4) Yayagala obwenkanya. Yasunguwala bwe yalaba abasuubuzi abaali bafuna amagoba mu bantu mu ngeri etaali ya bwenkanya. (Matayo 21:12, 13) Abaavu n’abanyigirizibwa yabayisa mu ngeri ey’obwenkanya, ng’abayamba ‘okufuna ekiwummulo.’ (Matayo 11:4, 5, 28-30) Amagezi ag’ekitalo geeyolekera mu njigiriza za Yesu, ‘eyali asinga Sulemaani.’ (Matayo 12:42) Naye Yesu teyeeraga olw’amagezi ge. Ebigambo bye yayogera byatuuka ku mitima gy’abantu aba bulijjo, kubanga bye yayigirizanga byali bitegeerekeka bulungi, nga byangu era nga bya mugaso.
19, 20. (a) Yesu yali atya ekyokulabirako eky’enkukunala mu kulaga okwagala? (b) Twandijjukiddenga ki nga tusoma era nga tufumiitiriza ku kyokulabirako kya Yesu?
19 Yesu yali kyakulabirako eky’enkukunala mu kulaga okwagala. Mu buweereza bwe bwonna, yayoleka okwagala mu ngeri ez’enjawulo, nga mw’otwalidde okulaga obusaasizi n’okufaayo ku balala. Yasaasiranga abaali babonaabona. Enfunda n’enfunda, okufaayo ku balala kwamukubiriza okubaako ky’akolawo okubayamba. (Matayo 14:14) Wadde yawonya abalwadde n’aliisa abalumwa enjala, Yesu yasaasira abantu mu ngeri esingawo obukulu. Yayamba abalala okumanya, okukkiriza, era n’okwagala amazima agakwata ku Bwakabaka bwa Katonda, obujja okuleetera abantu emikisa egy’olubeerera. (Makko 6:34; Lukka 4:43) Okusinga byonna, Yesu yayoleka okwagala okw’okwerekereza ng’awaayo obulamu bwe ku lw’abalala.—Yokaana 15:13.
20 Kyewuunyisa nti abantu ab’emyaka gyonna era abaava mu mbeera ez’enjawulo baasikirizibwa eri omusajja ono eyalaga okwagala n’obusaasizi? (Makko 10:13-16) Bwe tuba tusoma era nga tufumiitiriza ku kyokulabirako kya Yesu, tujjukirenga nti okuyitira mu Mwana oyo tuba tulaba Kitaawe.—Abaebbulaniya 1:3.
21, 22. Okunoonya Yakuwa kizingiramu ki, era ekitabo kino kirimu biki ebiyinza okutuyamba mu nsonga eyo?
21 Ng’atutegeereza ddala ky’ali mu Kigambo kye, Yakuwa akiraga bulungi nti ayagala tubeere n’enkolagana ennungi naye. Mu kiseera kye kimu, tatukaka kubeera na nkolagana nnungi naye. Kiri eri ffe okunoonya Yakuwa ‘ng’akyayinzika okulabika.’ (Isaaya 55:6) Okunoonya Yakuwa kizingiramu okumanya engeri ze n’amakubo ge ebitegeezebwa mu Baibuli. Ekitabo kino ky’osoma kati kitegekeddwa okukuyamba mu nsonga eyo.
22 Ojja kulaba nti ekitabo kino kyawuddwamu ebitundu ebyogera ku ngeri za Yakuwa enkulu ennya: amaanyi, obwenkanya, amagezi, n’okwagala. Buli kitundu kitandika nga kyogera mu bufunze ku ngeri eyeekenneenyezebwa. Essuula eziri mu kitundu ekyo zoogera ku ngeri ez’enjawulo Yakuwa gy’ayolekamu engeri eyo. Era buli kitundu kirimu essuula eraga Yesu bwe yalagamu engeri eyo, awamu n’essuula eraga bwe tuyinza okwolekamu engeri y’emu mu bulamu bwaffe.
23, 24. (a) Nnyonnyola engeri y’okukozesaamu akatundu ak’enjawulo akalina omutwe “Ebibuuzo eby’Okufumiitirizaako.” (b) Okufumiitiriza kutuyamba kutya okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Katonda?
23 Okutandika n’essuula eno, waliwo akatundu ak’enjawulo akalina omutwe “Ebibuuzo eby’Okufumiitirizaako.” Ng’ekyokulabirako, laba akasanduuko akali ku lupapula 24. Ebyawandiikibwa n’ebibuuzo ebikalimu tebitegekeddwa kukuyamba kwejjukanya ssuula gy’osomye. Wabula, ekigendererwa kyabyo kwe kukuyamba okufumiitiriza ku nsonga endala enkulu ezikwata ku mutwe ogwogerwako. Oyinza otya okukozesa obulungi akatundu ako? Kebera buli kyawandiikibwa ekiweereddwa, era soma ennyiriri ezo n’obwegendereza. Oluvannyuma, wekkaanye ekibuuzo ekiddiridde buli kyawandiikibwa. Lowooza ku by’okuddamu. Oyinza okunoonyereza ku ky’osomye. Weebuuze ebibuuzo nga bino: ‘Ebintu bino bintegeeza ki ku Yakuwa? Bikwata bitya ku bulamu bwange? Nnyinza ntya okubikozesa okuyamba abalala?
24 Okufumiitiriza mu ngeri eyo kiyinza okutuyamba okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Yakuwa. Lwaki? Kubanga Baibuli ekwataganya okufumiitiriza n’omutima. (Zabbuli 19:14, NW) Bwe tufumiitiriza ku bye tuyiga ku Katonda, bituuka ku mutima gwaffe ogw’akabonero, ne bikwata ku ndowooza zaffe, enneewulira zaffe, era ne bitukubiriza okubaako ne kye tukola. Okwagala kwaffe eri Katonda kweyongera, era ne kutukubiriza okwagala okumusanyusa nga Mukwano gwaffe ow’oku lusegere ennyo. (1 Yokaana 5:3) Okusobola okufuna enkolagana ng’eyo, tuteekwa okumanya engeri za Yakuwa n’amakubo ge. Kyokka, ka tusooke twekkaanye engeri ya Katonda etukubiriza okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi naye—obutukuvu bwe.
^ lup. 3 Ekyewuunyisa, ekigambo ky’Olwebbulaniya wano ekikyusiddwa “omukwano,” kikozesebwa mu Amosi 3:7, olugamba nti Mukama Afuga Byonna, Yakuwa, abikkula ‘eby’ekyama’ eri abaweereza be, ng’abategeeza nga bukyali ky’ateekateeka okukola.
^ lup. 12 Ng’ekyokulabirako, Baibuli eyogera ku maaso ga Katonda, amatu ge, ennyindo ze, akamwa ke, emikono gye n’ebigere. (Zabbuli 18:15; 27:8; 44:3; Isaaya 60:13; Matayo 4:4; 1 Peetero 3:12) Ebigambo ng’ebyo tetuyinza kubitwala nga bwe biri, era nga bwe tutayinza kutwala Yakuwa okuba ‘Olwazi’ oba ‘engabo,’ ng’ebigambo ebyo bimukozeseddwako.—Ekyamateeka 32:4; Zabbuli 84:11.
Ebibuuzo eby’Okufumiitirizaako
Zabbuli 15:1-5 Yakuwa asuubira ki okuva eri abo abaagala okubeera mikwano gye?
Zabbuli 34:1-18 Yakuwa ali kumpi ne baani, era abalinga abo bayinza kuba na bwesige ki?
Zabbuli 145:18-21 Biki ebinaatusobozesa okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Yakuwa?
2 Abakkolinso 6:14–7:1 Tulina kweyisa tutya bwe tuba ab’okubeera n’enkolagana ennungi ne Yakuwa?

Abalamazi Bakulunguka Okugenda Ewafiira Omujulizi Yowana Maria, Y’ani Ono?

2 Aris Marghetis 1890 BYE BYE BYE BYE bye 4.5

Bukedde Online - Namasole ayanukudde Buganda ku ttaka lye bakaayanira ne Kibuule
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
Omwoleso gwa 'Bride and Grom' 2018 gugguddwaawo e Lugogo: Gwakumala...
Bp. Ssekamaanya akuutidde ab’e Bungereza okubeera obumu
BUKEDDE W’OLWOMUKAAGA YATUUSE DDA MU KATALE NG’ALIMU GANO
Ekibuga Kabaka ky'azimbye e Kigo kikulidde...
Namasole Sarah Natoolo ayanukudde munnamateeka w’ekitongole kya Buganda Land Board (BLB), Denis Buyaga nti ettaka minisita omubeezi ow’amazzi Ronald Kibuule kwe yafuna ekyapa lya Bwannamasole era be balivunaanyizibwako ku lw’Obwakabaka bwa Buganda.
Natoolo 96, yategeezezza bannamawulire mu lubiri lwe e Lukuli - Lusaka mu Munisipaali y’e Makindye nti lino ettaka lirye lya bwannannyini era lyamuweebwa Evelyne Kulabako, maama wa Ssekabaka Daudi Chwa.
“Ettaka lino lisangibwa Koko- Kigaya mu ggombolola y’e Najja mu disitulikiti y’e Buikwe nga liwezaako mayiro ezisukka mu ssatu nga baalimpa mu 1938 nga sinnafuuka Namasole.
N’olwekyo ettaka eryo lyange ng’omuntu so ssi nga Namasole,” Natoolo bwe yannyonnyodde.
Wabula yeesammudde ebigambibwa nti ye yaguza Minisita Kibuule yiika 85, zaagobako abantu.
Yategeezezza nti lyatundibwa Musa Kateregga, gwe yali alitaddeko okulikuuma oluvannyuma lw’okukozesa olukujjukujju n’alifunako ekyapa okuva mu ofiisi y’eby’ettaka e Mukono.
Buyaga ng’ali mu lukiiko lw’abamawulire wiiki ewedde yategeezza ng’ekyapa Kibuule ky’alina ku ttaka ly’e Kigaya bwe kiri ekifu ng’ettaka lino lya Bwannalinnya era aba BLB be balivunaanyizibwako nga tebamumanyi era tebamuwangako kyapa.
Samuel Muyanja, avunaanyizibwa ku nzirukanya y’ebintu bya Namasole Natoolo e Kyaggwe yagambye nti Kateregga oluvannyuma lw’okukuuma ettaka lino ebbanga eddene, yakozesa olukujukujju n’alifunako ekyapa.
“Bwe twategeera nti Kateregga afunye ekyapa mu bukyamu twasalawo okumutwala mu kkooti ng’ensonga zaali tezinnaggwa ne batandika okusenda. Bwe twababuuza eyali abasindise ne bagamba nti Kibuule ng’ono ye yagula ku Kateregga.
Muyanja yagambye nti baatuuza olukiiko ku ttaka ne Minisita Kibuule mwe yali n’abalaga empapula zonna ze yagulirako nga n’ekyapa yakikyusa dda okuva mu mannya ga Kateregga n’akizza mu gage.
Yagambye nti tebakkaanya kwe kuddayo ne beebuuza ku munnamateeka waabwe n’abategeeza nga Kibuule bw’atalina musango gye bali kuba eyamuguza ekyapa kyali mu mannya ge.
“Yatugamba nti Kibuule mu mateeka aba ayitibwa “Bonafide purchaser by value” ng’era yatuwa amagezi okuvunaana Kateregga eyamuguza ng’era emisango gigenda mu maaso,” bwe yannyonnyodde.
Yagambye nti bwe baategeera ebyo abasenze abali ku ttaka Kibuule ly’alinako ekyapa ne babategeeza bakolagane naye kuba baali tebakyayinza kubamalira nsonga zaabwe.
Natolo yagambye nti Obwannamasole, bulina ettaka mayiro 20 ku kyalo Busagazi mu disitulikiti y’e Buikwe (Kyaggwe bbuloka 377 poloti 6) nga lino si lye lyogerwako.
Yagambye nti ettaka lino Namasole takkirizibwa kulitunda okuggyako okulifunako obusuulu okuva mu basenze ababa baliriko obumuyamba okwebezaawo n’abantu be kuba talina mpeera gy’afuna nga Nnamasole.
Ate Muyanja yalaze ebbaluwa gye yagambye nti yawandiikibwa minisita w’eby’obuwangwa n’ennono e Mengo Yusuf Ggaganga ku bw’Obwakabaka bwa Buganda ng’eragira ab’ekitongole ky’ettaka e Mukono okukolera Kateregga ekyapa kya yiika 400 ku ttaka ly’e Busagazi nti Namasole Natolo ye yazimuwa ekitali kituufu.
Ebbaluwa eno eyawandiikibwa nga May 22, 2016 eri omuwandiisi w’ebyapa ku disitulikiti y’e Mukono ng’amugamba nti yali afunye endagaano eyali ekoleddwa Namasole Natolo ng’awa Kateregga yiika 400 ku ttaka ly’e Busagazi.
Minisita Kibuule mu lukiiko lw’amawulire lwe yatuuza wiiki ewedde yategeeza nga bwe yakebera ebikwata ku ttaka lino mu ofiisi y’ettaka e Mukono n’asanga nga waliyo ekyapa ekyali mu mannya ga Musa Kateregga.
Ku ttaka ly’e limu, nga October 8, Dickson Wasswa yafa olw’emirerembe egiryekusaako ng’abatuuze baategeeza nti ono okufa kwe kwava ku kutulugunyizibwa abaserikale.

ibakawathikibawa bathi n’omulimu abuyirire?
4. Ni kyahi ekya kanganaya ngoku Musa abya kw’omulimu wa Nyamuhanga? (Lebaya ekisasani ekiri okw’itsuka.)
5 Ni bandi bahi abo Yehova aha omulimu wiwe abuyirire erisondolha abandu biwe? E Biblia yikabugha yithi: “Yosua mughalha wa Nuni abya oswire n’ekirimu ky’amenge.” (Eryibuka Ebihano 34:9) “Omulimu w’Omwami Mukulhu mwahika oku Gideoni.” (Abatsweri 6:34) Kandi “omulimu w’Omwami Mukulhu mwahika oku Dawudi.” (1 Samweli 16:13) Abalhume bosi abo ibakasaba erihembwa omulimu abuyirire wa Nyamuhanga eribathokesya erikolha ebya bathe banga thoka erikolha ahabwabu. (Yosua 11:16, 17; Abatsweri 7:7, 22; 1 Samweli 17:37, 50) Ekyo kikakanganaya ngoku Yehova yuwabya akabaha obuthoki bw’erikolha ebindu ebikaswekaya, neryo iyutholere eripipwa.
13 Abasondoli b’ebindi bihanda ibakaghendera okwa menge w’abandu. Ng’eky’erileberyako, abasondoli b’Abanya Kanana n’abandu babu ibakakolha eby’esyonye kutsibu, ng’erikolha emyatsi y’engyingo n’abawubuthenwe nabu, omukali n’omukali kutse omulhume n’omulhume, okwa bisoro, erihonga abaana, n’eriramya esyosanamu. (Abalawi 18:6, 21-25) N’abasondoli be Babeli ne Misiri sibabya bawithe ebihano by’obuyonzo ng’eby’abandu ba Nyamuhanga. (Emiganzo 19:13) Aliwe abandu ba Nyamuhanga ibo ibakabya ibanemulhangira abasondoli babu bakabahabulha eribya bayonzo kinyamubiri, n’omw’iramya, n’eriyihighulha okw’iyikinia omwa by’obusingiri. Kikalhangirika kwenene ku ni Yehova oyuwabya akabasondolha.
16. Ni kyahi ekya kanganaya ngoku omulimu abuyirire w’aha Yesu y’obuthoki?
16 Omulimu abuyirire mwaha Yesu y’obuthoki. Yesu abere akabatizibwa, Yoane mwalhangira ‘olhubulha lhukakyungukalha n’omulimu akamwandaghalirako ng’akalikuku.’ Neryo-neryo, “omulimu mwamulholya omo mbwarara.” (Mariko 1:10-12) Yesu iniane mukolha omubiiri wiwe w’okwa kihugho, omulimu abuyirire wa Nyamuhanga mwamuha obuthoki bw’erikangirirya n’erikolha ebithiko-thiko. (Emibiri 10:38) Kandi omulimu mwawathikya Yesu erithoka erikangania emibere ng’olhwanzo, obutseme, n’erikirirya erighumire. (Yoane 15:9; Abaebrania 12:2) Sihali wundi musondoli oyuwalhangirika kw’obuthoki ng’obo busana n’omulimu abuyirire. Ekyo kwesi mukyakangania ndeke-ndeke ngoku Yehova abya iniabirimusombolha eribya Musondoli.
AKALEBERYO—EY’ERIGHA

Baddereeva bagamba nti baakedde bulungi kyokka kyababuuseko nga waliwo bannaabwe abagguddewo siteegi okumpi n’oluguudo nga bano bazze n’ekibinja ky’abayaaye era olwabagambyeko ne batandika okubakuba emiggo n’amayinja ekyawalirizza Poliisi okukuba amasasi mu bbanga saako omukka ogubalagala okukakkanya embeera.
Baddereeva balumiriza Meeya w’e Kawempe, Dr. Emmanuel Sserunjoji okuba emabega w’obutabanguko buno. Bagamba yaleeta enganda ze okutandikawo siteegi ezikontana n’amateeka.
Wabula Sserunjogi bino byonna abiwakanyizza n’ategeeza nti baddereeva basaanye okwewala okuwubisibwa bannabyabufuuzi abataagala nkulaakulana mu kitundu.

Ssente z’okuzimba ssweeta y’e Kamuli zikwasizza yinginiya
Dokita w'amagumba bamuggalidde lwa kubba...
Obwakyabazinga bwa kusimba ku nsonga 6 mu...
ABATUUZE b’omu ggombolola y’e Kisozi mu disitulikiti y’e Kamuli batabukidde abakungu ba disitulikiti olw’okufuula pulojekiti y’okuzimba ssweeta y’eddwaaliro ly’e Nankandulo ey’okulyamu ssente nga kati emyaka 14 nga bagizimba.
Ssweeta emaze emyaka egisoba mu 10 ng’ezimbibwa.
Ekyewuunyisa buli mwaka baliko omutemwa gwe bafuna okuva ku bajeti kyokka nga ssweeta ekyagaanyi okuggwa. Ssweeta yatandika okuzimbibwa mu 2002.
Gavumenti yasalawo okugaziya eddwaaliro lya Nankandulo Health Center 1V, abantu bawone okugenda e Kamuli nga baali batambula mayiro ezisoba mu 30.
Mu 2002, baali basuubira okusaasaanya obukadde 75 okuzimba ssweeta kyokka baakagissaako obukadde 300, kuba buli mwaka bayinginiya basaba ssente endala.
Mu kiseera kino abantu abagenda okubalongoosa batwalibwa mu ddwaaliro e Kamuli, Jinja ne Iganga.
Kansala wa Kisozi ku disitulikiti Charles Mpalabule azze yeemulugunya olw’omulimu okutambula akasoobo nga n’omutindo tegumatiza wabula nga bayinginiya bakakasa nti kye bakola bakimanyi kuba kye baasoma.
Abakungu okuva mu ofi isi ya Pulezidenti abalondoola ebyobulamu bwe kyagenze e Kamuli abatuuze baabaloopedde engeri ssweeta eno gye yagaana okumalirizibwa era nga ne Salama Musumba bwe yali Ssentebe yagezaako okubalaga eky’okukola kyokka ne bagaana.
Mulondo nga y’omu kuba naggagga b’e Kamuli yatuuyanidde mu kaduukulu wakati mu kulaajana bamuyimbule ku kakalu ka poliisi.
Abatuuze bagamba nti amadiriisa galinga ag’ennyumba esulwamu nga ne bw’obeera ebweru olaba bulungi ebiri munda.
Ate n’engeri enzigi gye zaakolebwamu si yabuwangaazi kuba tebassaako byuma bikuuma bintu biri munda newankubadde nga bakimanyi nti byamuwendo abantu basobola okubibba.
Nabatanzi yabadde n’omubaka wa Pulezidenti e Kamuli Moses Ddumba. Nabatanzi yagambye nti bagenda kutwala abakugu bakebere engeri ssweeta eno gye yazimbibwamu n’okubalirira ensaasaanya ya ssente balabe ekiddirira.

Luganda – ki kati

Yee. Dayimooni be “bamalayika abaayonoona;” bitonde bya mwoyo ebyajeemera Katonda. (2 Peetero 2:4) Malayika eyasooka okufuuka dayimooni ye Sitaani Omulyolyomi, Bayibuli gy’eyita “omufuzi wa badayimooni.”—Matayo 12:24, 26.
Obujeemu mu biseera bya Nuuwa
Bayibuli eyogera ku bamalayika abaajeemera Katonda ng’Amataba g’omu biseera bya Nuuwa tegannabaawo. Egamba nti: “Abaana ba Katonda ow’amazima ne balaba ng’abawala b’abantu balabika bulungi, era ne batandika okuwasa bonna be baalondangamu.” (Olubereberye 6:2) Bamalayika abo ababi ‘baaleka ebifo byabwe ebituufu bye baalina okubeeramu’ mu ggulu ne beeyambaza emibiri gy’abantu basobole okwegatta nabo.—Yuda 6.
Amataba bwe gajja, bamalayika abo abajeemu beeyambulako emibiri gye baali beeyambazza ne baddayo mu ggulu. Naye Katonda yabagoba mu maka ge ag’omu ggulu. Ekimu ku bibonerezo Katonda bye yabawa kwe kubagaana okuddamu okweyambaza emibiri gy’abantu.—Abeefeso 6:11, 12.

Bukedde Online - Ekyama ky'amaanyi ga Bobi Wine kiri waka
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
Bawera kusimbira kkuuli enteekateeka z'okuyisa bajeti ya 2018/2019...
Ekyama ky'amaanyi ga Bobi Wine kiri waka
Zitujja eηηoma zitujja lwa ssente .... ez’abavubuka!...
Ensibuko y’obubbi mu masomero yeetaaga okuzibikira...
By Kizito Musoke
YAZAALIBWA ye Robert Kyagulanyi Ssentamu bwe yatandika okuyimba ne yeegattako erya His Excellence Bobi Wine, Omubanda wa Kabaka , kati ate abalonzi b’e Kyaddondo East baamugasseeko erinnya lya “Taata Kyaddondo” ne mukyala we Barbie Itungo Kyagulanyi ne bamubatirizaawo erya “Maama Kyaddondo” .
Bakyagulanyi lwe baatongoza ekitabo kya Barbie ate waggulu ge maka gaabwe agasangibwa e Magere.
Abafumbo bano balaze ebiwonvu n’ebikko bye bazze bayitamu okuva lwe baasisinkana emyaka 16 emabega n’engeri gye bazze beenyigira mu mirimu gye bakola okutuusa abalonzi lwe baabongeddeko “amayinja”.
Obutafaanana ng’obufumbo bwa basereebu obuzze buyuuga era ne bulabibwa ne mu mawulire, obwa bano bubadde bwa njawulo olw’okuba bateredde ntende.
Bano balina ne pulogulaamu gye baali bakola bombi emanyiddwa nga Ghetto President Reality TV Show.
Ekibiina kya Twaweza kyalonda Bobi Wine okubeera ambasada waakyo olw’okubeera taata ow’obuvunaanyizibwa.
Wadde ng’abafumbo bano tebali mu mbeera mbi mu byenfuna, kyokka bakuza abaana baabwe mu mbeera nga basobola okukola emirimu.
Bw’oyingira mu maka gaabwe agasangibwa ku kyalo Magere oyanirizibwa olusuku awamu n’ebiyumba mwe balundira ebisolo awaka.
Bobi Wine ne famire nga bava mu nnimiro. Okukolera awamu g’emaanyi.
Barbie Kyagulanyi alaze engeri gye beezimbye n’omukono gwe mu buwanguzi bwa bba.
“ Twalabagana mu 2001 ne tugattibwa mu bufumbo obutukuvu mu 2011 mu Lutikko e Lubaga. Twasooka kubeera baamukwano nga tuzannya fenna omuzannyo ku National Theatre.
Mu kiseera ekyo waaliwo abantu abaali bataagala tubeere fenna nga balaba tuyinza obutakwatagana bulungi olw’embeera zaffe.
Kyokka olw’okuba nze nali nsiimye omulenzi wange ssaabawuliriza okutuusa nabo lwe baabivaako nga balaba tutambula bulungi.
Okusooka embeera teyali nnungi, era kyali kyetaagisa okukola emirimu egiwerako okusobola okufuna ssente.
Emirimu egisinga baze gy’akoze mbadde ngyenyigiramu butereevu n’okutuusa olwaleero.
E Busaabala mbeerayo nnyo nga tukwasaganya emirimu era kitukoledde.
Bobi ne baganda be batambulira wamu nga famire ne mu kampeyini bwe kibadde.
KYAGULANYI NGA TAATA
Baze ye musajja w’ekirooto kyange era taata ow’obuvunaanyizibwa eri abaana. Akoze kinene okukyusa ebiseera byange eby’omu maaso kuba ankozeemu omulimu ne nfuuka ow’omugaso.
Okubeera mukyala wa Bobi Wine kinyambye okumanyibwa abantu bangi n’emikwano.
Olumu ebizibu by’afuna nange babinteekamu naddala mu kisaawe ky’okuyimba naye tubivvuunuka anti twafuuka omuntu omu.
Waliwo ebintu bye baba bakusuubiramu ng’oli muka sereebu, okugeza bakusuubira nti olina okuba omusanyufu ekiseera kyonna.
Bwe balaba nga toli nga batandika okwebuuza obuzibu obukuliko.
ENGERI BARBIE GY’ANOONYEZZA AKALULU
Akalulu kano okuva lwe kaatandika, mbadde sikyakola, nga nnina okuzuukuka ku ssaawa 11:00 ku makya ne ntegeka abaana ne bagenda ku ssomero.
Barbie Kyagulanyi abadde mpagi mu kampeyini. Wano ng’asaba abalonzi akalulu.
Ekirungi waliwo abantwalira, olwo ne nsobola okutegekera omwami omuli engoye z’agenda okwambala n’ekyokulya.
Ekiseera ky’okunoonya akalulu kikosezzaamu abaana bange kuba babadde balwayo ku ssomero, era nga sikyasobola kubalabirira nga bakola ebibaweereddwa ku ssomero kuba emirundi mingi mbadde nsangibwa siriiwo.
Mbadde nnina okukwasaganya buli mulimu gwonna nga muno mulimu okulaba nga mmanya ekifa mu bantu baffe ab’enjawulo, wamu n’okubaperereza batuwe akalulu.
Wadde mubaddemu ebigambo mu kampeyni, kyokka nga ffe tebituggya ku mulamwa. Bibadde tebirina kye bitukolako okuggyako okwesekera.
Bino tebyenkana bigambo biri mu kisaawe kya kuyimba era ffe tubadde tusangiddwa nga twaguma dda kuba eno y’embeera gye tuwangaaliddemu.
Ekirungi omwami wange musajja mugezi, mugumiikiriza, alumirirwa ekitundu kye awamu n’abantu bonna abali mu mbeera gye yavaamu.
Mutabani wabwe Kampala ng’akutte essaawa akabonero ke baakozesezza mu kunoonya akalulu.
Sirabawo kipya kuba tubadde tukolagana n’abantu bangi. Wadde ku luno tugenda kweyongerako abantu, kyokka tekijja kutumenya nnyo kuba si kipya. Kati kye nnina okulaba, y’engeri y’okugabanyaamu obudde.
Mu kiseera kino nsangiddwa nga nnina ekibiina ky’obwa nnakyewa ekya Caring Heart Uganda mwe tuyita okuyamba abaana abawala abatalina mwasirizi, n’abo abawanduka mu masomero olw’okubulwa fi izi.
Tutambulira wamu nga famire ekiseera kyonna era ne mu kalulu babadde tebandekerera.
Omukyala ambadde ku lusegere ssaawa bina olumu nga ne mutabani waffe Kampala atwegattako n’akwata akabonero kaffe ak’essaawa ng’akalaga abantu.
Kino kiraga nti tukolera wamu nga famire mu buli kimu era amaka bwe galina okuba okusobola okwekulaakulanya.
Ekiseera kyonna tebandekeredde, era nga tusaba fenna Katonda asobole okwanukula essaala zaffe.
Ekirungi kya famire yange bakimanyi nti ndi muyimbi era babadde tebansuubira kwambala masuuti.
Mpozzi kati nga bwe nafuuse omubaka nja kugambala. Bwe nkomawo awaka mbeera taata wa baana, so si muyimbi gwe mumanyi”.
Mu kiseera kino Bobi Wine ali mu mwaka ogusooka, asoma byamateeka ku Cavendish University. Ng’oggyeeko okuyimba, musajja mulimi era musuubuzi.
Bobi Wine yagambye nti okusinga byalinga bizibu bya ssente bye byamuleetera okukyusanga amasomero buli kaseera. Yasomera mu masomero 12.
Tulina ennimiro awaka era tugenda ffenna n’abaana ne tulima, buli lwe tubeera n’obudde.
Kino nkikola kuba njagala bakule nga bamanyi obulamu bw’abantu baabulijjo, nga tebeeyibaala nti baana ba sereebu.
Nga taata ow’obuvunaanyizibwa, nnina okuyigiriza abaana okweyimirizaawo mu nsi.
Ewaffe mmange teyalina ssente, kyokka yafuba okunjigiriza emirimu egy’enjawulo era kinnyambye mu bulamu.
Abaana bange simanyi kye balibeera enkya, noolwekyo nnina okubaluηηamya nga nkyaliwo.
Tuyigirizza abaana okulima era emmere esinga tugiggya mu nnimiro yaffe awaka.
Abaana mbakuza nga mbateekamu eddiini era mu maka gange tukeera kusaba. Ku wiikendi tumanyi okugendako ku bbiici e Busaabala ne banyumirwa”

Bukedde Online - ‘Bannaddiini muve ku by’omwoyo byokka muluhhanye ne ku byenfuna’
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
Bawera kusimbira kkuuli enteekateeka z'okuyisa bajeti ya 2018/2019...
‘Bannaddiini muve ku by’omwoyo byokka muluhhanye ne ku byenfuna’
ABAKULEMBEZE b’eddiini mu ggwanga basiimiddwa olw’okujjumbira enkola ezigenderera okusitula ebyenfuna y’abantu nga balwanyisa obwavu.
Kalisa (ku kkono), Bp. Mwesigwa nga basala akaguwa okuggulawo ettabi lya SACCO.
Okusiima kuno kwabadde mu bubaka bwa minisita w’ensonga za Karamoja, Ying. John Byabagambi bwe yatisse omubaka w’e Ibanda, Jovline Kalisa Kyomukama eyamukiikiridde ku mukolo gw’okuggulawo ettabi lya SACCO mu kibuga Ibanda ku Lwokutaano.
Muky Kalisa yategeezezza nti bannaddiini tebalina kukoma ku kulyowa myoyo gyokka wabula n’okubaako engeri gye baluh− hamya abantu mu kulwanyisa obwavu.
Yannyonnyodde nti olw’okwagala okulaba ng’abantu bava mu bwavu, Pulezidenti ky’avudde assa essira mu kulaba nga bbanka y’okwekulaakulanya Uganda Development Bank n’ebibiina by’obwegassi byongerwamu amaanyi n’okulaba nga bbanka y’obwegassi eya Cooperative Bank eddawo.
Muky. Kalisa yasiimye omulabirizi wa Ankole, Dr Sheldon Mwesigwa okutandika enkola y’okulaba ng’abatuuze batandikawo SACCO.
Omulabirizi mu kwogera kwe yeebazizza bakulembeze banne olw’okubeerawo kyokka n’awabula nti ekisinze okusuula SACCO be bakulembeze abadda ku nsimbi eziterekebwa bammemba ne bazeekomya.
Yategeezezza nti musanyufu olw’okuba bukya yatandikawo nkola ya kulwanyisa bwavu okuyita mu nkola y’okutereka n’okwewola, Pulezidenti yabasuula omukono n’obukadde 300 okutambuza enkola eno.
Minisita Byabagambi ne Muky. Kalisa buli omu yawaddeyo obukadde 3 okugenda mu ggwanika lya SACCO.

Bukedde Online - Eyakazaala abalongo emirundi 5 omusajja amudduseeko nebimusobera ne kumizigo tebamwetaaga
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
Lwaki abasajja abamu tebaagala mbeerera?
Desire alumbye abasajja abaanika eby’omu...
Amaze emyaka 10 ng’anobye mu ddya akomeddewo...
OKUZAALA kujaagaana, nga gw’ozaalidde, abaana abaagala. Tunuulira Sumayiya Nakanwagi, eyaakazaala abalongo emirundi etaano. Bba yamuddukako ng’amuyita wa bisiraani olw’okuzaala abalongo abataggwa ate ne ku mayumba kw’asula abundabunda bubunzibunzi nga bamugoba olw’abaana.
Nakanwagi, nga mutuuze w’e Wakaliga, bukya atandika kuzaala, azaala balongo bokka nga yaakazaala abalongo emirundi etaano. Erya Nnaalongo lyamuvaako dda kuba bw’ozaala abalongo emirundi egisukka ebiri ng’erinnya olifiirwa.
Wadde ng’abantu bangi baagala okuzaala abalongo naye ye Nakanwagi ebizibu by’abafuniddemu bimwerabizza essanyu ly’obweza. Edda baagambanga nti Nnaalongo tanoba era nti oli bw’azaala abalongo ng’obufumbo bunywedde, naye ate obwa Nakanwagi bwafa bufi.
Ennaku gy’ayitamu agirojja bw’ati:
Ng’omuwala yenna bw’akula n’asuubira okufuna omusajja afumbirwe bakole amaka era bafune n’abaana babeere basanyufu nange bwe nali era olwavubuka ne ntandika okukola bizinensi nga ndi musuubuzi wa matooke ge naggyanga e Mbarara ne ngatunda mu Kampala e Busega ne Nateete.
Mu 2002, nafuna baze, Jamir Batte era ne tusalawo okubeera ffenna, ekiseera kyatuuka emirimu gye ne gicankalana olwo nga nze mbeezaawo awaka. Namugulira pikipiki akole bodaboda tusobole okubeera obulungi. Mu 2003, twateesa tuzaale era mu 2004 ne nkubawo abaana abasooka Wasswa ne Nakato. Kino kyasanyusa nnyo baze omukwano gwaffe ne gweyongera.
Mu 2012 nga ndi lubuto lw’abalongo abookusatu, natandika okufunamu obutakkaanya n’omwami wange nga buli ssente gye nkola gy’atwala wabula ne tubeerawo bwe tutyo. Mu 2013 nga mmaze okuzaala abalongo abookuna nagenda
okusuubula amatooke e Mbarara nga bwe nakolanga ne bankubira essimu nga mmange mulwadde nnyo. Emmotoka nagiweereza baze atunde emmere era eno yagitunda ssente zonna n’azitwala. Kuno yagattako okusiba ebintu by’omu nnyumba byonna ne poloti ze nali nguze yazitunda n’antegeeza nti ababbi baali batulumbye ne batubba ebintu byonna. Baliraanwa be banzibirako nti ye yali abitutte.
Natandika okumubanja ebintu byange ne ssente olwo ne yeecanga n’ayagala n’okunjokera mu nnyumba n’abaana baffe era n’annangira ebintu bingi ng’abaana abalongo bwe babeera n’ebisiraani nga ye takyasobola kubeera nange. Okuva olwo siddangamu kumulaba wadde okumuwuliza.
Abehhanda za baze nabo beegatta ku baze ne bampalana nga bwe bampita ow’ebisiraani olw’okuzaalanga abalongo buli kiseera nti era bo tebakirabangako mu kika kyabwe.
Eky’okundekawo sandikifuddeko naye ate omusajja yanzibako buli kintu ne kapito yamubba ne mba nga sirina we ntandikira.
Omwami ng’amaze okugenda nafuna obulwadde obw’amaanyi ne bahhamba bwa kinnansi era nagenda ew’omusawo w’ekinnansi Charles Ssebusolo era eno banzijanjaba. Bantegeeza nti nali nninamu olubuto lwa myezi ebiri nga baze yalunsibirako luleme kukula nti era lwali lugenda kuvundira munda nga nali nneetaaga kwegatta n’omusajja aluzuukuse. Omusawo ono yantegeeza nti ye yekka asobola okukola kino.
Olw’okuba nali mu kusoberwa nga ndaba hhenda kufa nakkiriza era yankozesa olubuto ne lukula kyokka n’antegeeza nti lwali lwa baze.
Nagenda okubala emyezi nga ndaba ky’ekiseera omusawo ono we yanzijanjabira era nalwo nazaala balongo Babirye ne Kato wabula Kato n’abuuka. Omusawo ono naye abaana be abakulu bantabukira ne bangoba ne mu nnyumba gye yali ampadde okubeeramu ne nzira mu muzigo.
ENGERI GYE MBAAWULA
Olw’okuba abalongo nnina bangi buli omu alina erinnya ery’enjawulo lye namwongerako kwe mmwawulira okugeza nnina Wasswa Mukulu, Wasswa Bbiri, Wasswa Mutono, Nakato Mukulu, Kato Mukulu era gano ge nsinga okukozesa okwawula ani gwe mpita.
NGOBAGANYIZIBWA KU NJU LWA BAANA
Obuzibu bwe mpitamu bungi okukuza abaana bange omunaana kuba kati sikyakola wabula ndiwo ku lwa bazirakisa anti abaana sirina gwe nsobola kubalekera. Bannannyini mayumba bannangira buli kakedde ng’abaana bwe bajjuza kaabuyonjo zaabwe era kati we nsula kaabuyonjo baagihhaana, napangisa ya mu katale k’abaazirwanako e Wakaliga gye tugenda okweyamba n’abaana bange.
Charles Ssebusolo omusawo w’ekinnansi Taata w'abalongo abasembyeyo
.Olumu waaliwo omukazi eyali abbye abaana basatu ng’abateeka mu mmotoka agenda nabo wabula abantu olwalengera yunifoomu ne bamulemesa nga bategedde nti eyo yunifoomu y’abaana bange.
Essaawa eno nnina obuzibu nti ku mayumba kwe mbeera bangobaganya olw’okubeera n’abaana abangi ate ennyumba yange gye nali ntandise okuzimbako nayo yasigala awo kuba sikyalina kye nkola. Nze taata nze maama, abalongo abakulu babadde basoma mu kibiina kyakusatu wabula sinnafuna ssente zibazzaayo ku ssomero era bali waka batudde. Ekyokulya nakyo kizibu kuba kati mbeerawo ku lwa bazirakisa abampa ng’abasinga ba mizikiti be bannyamba okumbeezaawo.
OMUSAJJA ANTADDE KU BUNKENKE
Ekinneeraliikiriza, waliwo abantu abatandise okunnondoola be nteebereza okuba nti basindikibwa eyali baze. Oluusi nfuna abankubira essimu nti bampadde obuyambi bw’abaana hhende mbunone wabula hhenda okutuukayo nga tewali muntu ate bwe nziramu okukuba essimu eyo eba teriiko ekitadde obulamu bwange mu matigga.
Omwami wange bwe yamala okunzibako ebyange byonna yagezaako okunzita ng’ayagala kunjokera mu nnyumba, ssente zonna ze yabba nazivaako njagala kukuza baana bange era namwesonyiwa simanyi ate lwaki annondoola.
Dr. Othniel Musana omusawo w’abakyala ku ddwaaliro e Nsambya agamba: Omukyala okuzaala aba¬longo buli luzaalo kitegeeza nti akola amagi mangi nga buli mwezi afulumya eggi erisukka mu limu. Omukyala ow’ekikula kino kizibu okuzaala omwana omu, wabula ate era nga kisobo¬ka naye kirwawo olw’obungi bw’amagi g’alina. Omukyala bulijjo afulumya eggi limu buli mwezi naye ono ow’ekikula kino afulumya amagi mangi.
Omukyala ow’engeri eno ayinza okuba ng’embeera gy’alimu ya nsikirano ng’obutonde bwe bulina omuntu gwe bugoberera mu kika naddala ku ludda lwa maama we kale nakyo kireetera embeera eyo.”
Ate Sahaban Waibango. ssentebe w’abasawo b’ekinnansi mu munisi¬paali y’e Lubaga agamba: Okuzaala abalongo emirundi emingi kya mirundi ebiri okufuna emikisa oba ebisiraani, okugaggawala oba okwavuwala. Abalongo kintu kizibu nnyo era balina emizizo mingi, mu nkola y’ekinnansi abalongo balina eddagala lye banaaba ne bakolebwako emizizo ne batereera.

Ffe ne tuyimba ‘Agawalaggana mu nkoola’, nabo ne bakuba ebivuga byabwe okusinziira ku bwe tuyimba, ne tuzina, abawagizi ne banyumirwa oluyimba! Kino abayimbi ba Uganda kye batannayiga, baagala kufaanana nga Bazungu sso ng’ebyabwe tebabisobola.

Amagendo kye ki?
Amagendo by’ebyamaguzi ebikukuse okuyingira oba okufuluma eggwanga. Abeenyigiranga mu busuubuzi bw’ebyamaguzi bino ebikukuse nga bayitibwa Bannamagendo nga n’omulimu gwo baguyita kukola magendo. Amagendo era gaabanga ebintu ebitakkirizibwa kutundibwa oba omuntu ng’atunda ebintu by’atakkirizibwa kutunda.

OMUTENDESI wa KCCA , Mike Mutebi akkirizza nti Esperance yabayinze obuzito bwe baabadde battunka mu gwa CAF Champions League ku Lwokubiri.
Mutebi, yagambye nti abazannyi be baazannye bulungi ne bakulembera omupiira era nga basobola n’okuguwangula kyokka baatuuse ekiseera ne balemererwa olw’obutaba na bumanyirivu.
Mu ddakiika ey’e 17, Jackson Nunda yateebedde KCCA ggoolo eyasoose ng’omuzibizi wa Esperance, Khalil Chemmam tanneeteeba mu ddakiika ya 21.
Oluvannyuma lwa ggoolo zino KCCA teyazzeemu kufuna buweerero okutuusa Anice Badru ne Saad Bguirmu bwe baateebedde Esperance mu ddakiika eya 30 n’eya 37 olwo omupiira ne guwummula maliri (2-2).
“Twazannye bulungi kyokka obutaba na bumanyirivu kyatuyiyeeyo. Waliwo emikisa gye twafunye nga twetaaga kukozesa bukugu okugiteeba ne kitulema,” Mutebi bwe yategeezezza.
Ono yamugasseeko Patrick Kaddu mu kifo kya Muhammad Shaban ne Hassan Musana eyazze mu kya Allan Okello. Badding’ana Lwamukaaga lwa wiiki ejja nga July 28.
Wabula Mutebi yagambye nti bagulinamu enkizo kuba bagenda kubeera Namboole gye baakubira Al Ahly (2-0).
Yalabudde nti Esperance esuubire okusanga akaseera akazibu mu gw’okudding’ana kuba agenda kutereeza ensobi ezabakubizza era n’asuubiza abawagizi okubawa essanyu mu mupiira guno.

Ne kitegeeza nti abantu abalina obulwadde buno olumu bakola ebintu nga tebasoose na kulowooza ku bigenda kubaviiramu.
Okwagala okukola eby’obukaba olumu kyeyolekera mu kunyumya akaboozi, okwemazisa, okulaba ebifaananyi by’obuseegu, okugula bamalaaya n’okunyumyanyumya ebiboozi ebikwata ku kwegatta buli kiseera.
Kyokka eky’ennaku obulwadde buno bubadde tebunnafunirwa ddagala.
Okusinziira ku mukugu mu by’endwadde z’emitwe e Bungereza, Dr. Valerie Voon, okuva mu ttendekero lya Royal College of Psychiatrists abalina obulwadde buno tebatera kuvaayo kuba tebamanya nti balina ekikyamu ate era bibaswaza.
Yagambye nti alina essuubi nti obulwadde buno olumu bujja kutandika okujjanjabwa okugeza ng’okunakuwala n’okweraliikirira bwe baabifunira eddagala.

Enkola n’obukkwakkulizo
Empaka zino zitunuliira nokugezesa ebikwata ku ddembe lyobuntu mu nsi yonna.
Dr. Anthony Kakooza, Dean owa UCU Faculty of Law agambey nti buno bubadde buwanguzi bwenyini, bwebatuseeko nga bakoze bulungi.
UCU lye ttenedekero mu Uganda lyokka eryetabye mu mpaka zino, nga badiridde University of Pretoria, eya gwanga lya South Africa.
Eyasinze ye Colombia Law School mu America, oluvanyuma lwokufuna obubonero 3,209.

Abaana basaba emmere,+ naye tewali agibawa.+ ה [Ke] 5 Abaalyanga emmere ennungi bagalamidde mu nguudo olw’enjala.+ Abaakuzibwa nga bambala engoye ez’ebbeeyi*+ beebaka mu ntuumu z’evvu. ו [Wawu] 6 Ekibonerezo kya* muwala w’abantu bange kisinga ekibonerezo* ky’ekibuga Sodomu,+Ekyazikirizibwa mu kaseera obuseera nga tewali akiyamba.+ ז [Zayini] 7 Abanaziri+ ba Sayuuni baali balongoofu okusinga omuzira, baali beeru okusinga amata. Temuli balongoofu!” “Mugende eri! Temutukwatako!” Tebalina wa kubeera era babundabunda. Abantu tebajja kuwa bakabona kitiibwa+ wadde okulaga abakadde ekisa.”+ ע [Ayini] 17 Ne kaakano amaaso gaffe gakooye okunoonyeza obwereere obuyambi.+ Twanoonya obuyambi okuva eri eggwanga eryali litasobola kutulokola.+ צ [Sade] 18 Batuyigga buli we tugenda+ ne kiba nti tetusobola kutambulira mu bifo byaffe ebya lukale. Bukedde Online - By’olina okugenderera ng’olonda hositeero
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
Aga wano na wali
By’olina okugenderera ng’olonda hositeero
Abayizi mu kisulo.
Obutakaluubirirwa mu kusoma ku yunivasite olina okusula obulungi. Waliwo amatendekero agalina ebisulo wabula engeri abayizi gye babeera abangi abamu beesanga balina kusula mu hositeero. Ku Uganda Christian University omwogezi yunivasite Prim Kesande agamba nti abayizi abali mu mwaka ogusooka ku kitebe ekikulu e Mukono bonna balina okusulamu.
Ku balala ayagala yasulamu oba oyinza okufuna hositeero. Hannington Katumba omukozi mu ttendekero ly’e Makerere agamba nti abayizi abamu basula mu bisulo bya munda abalala ne beefunira hositeero.
Abayizi be bawadde ku Gavumenti nga ba kkoosi za ssaayansi abasinga babawa awokusula. Wabula abayizi tebabagaana kugenda kusula mu hositeero oba okuva awaka. Ku bayizi abeesasulira abamu baweebwa omukisa okwesasulira ne basula mu bisulo eby’omunda mu yunivasite wabula olina okuwaayo okusaba era ne bakuddamu oba bakukkiriza.
Mulimu abayizi abatayagala kusula na bannaabwe mu bisulo by’omunda oba mu hositeero. Bano basalawo kupangisa nnyumba nnamba. Wabula omwana ng’anaalonda wa wagenda okusula alina okubaako ebintu bye yekenneenya obutasanga buzibu.
By ’ogenderera ku hositee ro 1. Okufuna hositeero omuyizi alina okufuna eyo egya mu ssente z’asobola. Okugeza waliwo hositeero ezirimu buli kimu nga ne ttivvi ogisangamu.
Zino zitera okuba ez’ebbeeyi zitandikira ku mitwalo 90 okutuuka ku kakadde. Wabula waliwo n’eziri wakati wa 250,000/- ne 300,000/- buli lusoma.
2.Faayo okumanya ku byokwerinda bwe biyimiridde. Waliwo hositeero ezimu eziri okumpi n’ensiko nga kyangu abantu ababi okwekwekamu ne bababba. 3. Okufuna ekifo nga musobola okwetaasa ssinga ekizimbe kiba kikutte omuliro. Balina okussaamu ebizikiza omuliro ate ne babayigiriza n’okubikozesa.
4. Obuyonjo kintu kikulu ng’omuyizi anoonya hositeero. Sooka olambule ebiyigo ne kaabuyonjo oba bya mutindo.
Ebimu babissaako abakozi ababiyonja oba abayizi okwekolamu omulimu. Weetegereze oba amazzi weegali ate nga agebiyigo tebakulukutira ku ngulu.
5. Hositeero zibaako obusenge obw’egyenjawulo. Wabaawo akasenge nga kagendamu ekitanda kimu. Waliwo omuba ddeeka ng’eno oba osasula ssente ensaamusaamu.
6. Genderera oba waliwo we muyinza okwoleza engoye zammwe n’okwanika. Oba weetaaga okwetaaya obulungi.
7. Faayo ku ngeri gy’onoofunamu emmere. Waliwo hositeero nga zibateerawo awookufumbira. Ate waliwo ezitabakkiriza kufumba wabula nga waliwo we muyinza okugula emmere etali ya bulabe ku bulamu bwo.
8. Genderera abantu b’ogenda okusula nabo oba bakolaganika nabo ng’enneeyisa yaabwe ekwatagana n’eyiyo.
9. Waliwo hositeero ezirina amateeka. Zino ziba nnungi kuba ziyamba okusunsula mu baziyingiramu. Ezimu ziragira abayizi bonna okuba munda ku ssaawa 6:00 ez’ekiro. Waliwo ezabawala ezitakkiriza balenzi mu bisulo. Weegendereze hositeero ezitalina mateeka ezitalina afaayo kubanga zisuza n’abatali bayizi abayinza okuba n’ebigendererwa ebibi.
10.Genderera olugendo oluliwo okutuuka ku ttendekero.
11. Waliwo hositeero ezirina emmotoka ezitambuza abayizi okugenda okusoma n’okubazza.
12. Omuyizi olina okwewala okusasula nga tolina ndagaano gy’owandiise ne nnannyini hositeero baleme kukwegaana. Balina okukuwa lisiiti nayo gitereke bulungi.

Magazini y’Omunaala gw’Omukuumi, Na.2 2017 _ Osiima Ekirabo eky’Omuwendo Katonda kye Yakuwa?
Ku birabo byonna Katonda bye yali atuwadde, kiruwa ekisinga okuba oky’omuwendo?
Bayibuli egamba nti: “Katonda yayagala nnyo ensi n’awaayo Omwana we eyazaalibwa omu yekka.”—Yokaana 3:16.
Akatabo kano kannyonnyola ensonga lwaki Katonda yatuma Yesu ku nsi okutufiirira n’engeri gye tuyinza okulaga nti tusiima ekyo kye yatukolera.
Bayibuli eyogera ku kirabo ekisobozesa abo abakikkiriza okufuna obulamu obutaggwaawo. Eriyo ekirabo eky’omuwendo okusinga ekyo?
Lwaki Ekirabo Ekyo kya Muwendo Nnyo?
Biki ebifuula ekirabo ekimu omuba eky’omuwendo okusinga ebirala? Bwe tunaafumiitiriza ku bintu ebyo kijja kutuyamba okwongera okusiima ekinunulo.
Onoolaga otya nti osiima ekirabo Katonda kye yatuwa?
Amadiini agamu gagaana abakulembeze baago okuwasa. Naye Ebyawandiikibwa bigamba ki ku nsonga eno?
Mu biseera eby’edda, abantu ba Katonda baava mu buddu. Eky’ennaku abantu bangi bakyakozesebwa ng’abaddu.
Okuyamba kuganyula abalala era naawe kukuganyula. Kunyweza enkolagana wakati w’abantu. Oyinza otya okugaba n’essanyu?
Bayibuli egamba nti ‘mu nnaku ez’enkomerero, ebiseera biriba bizibu nnyo.’ Ebiseera ebyo bye bino bye tulimu?

Bukedde Online - Ebisoomooza ku mitendera egy’enjawulo mu bufumbo n’engeri y’okubivvuunuka
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
BUKEDDE W’OLWOMUKAAGA YATUUSE DDA MU KATALE NG’ALIMU BINO
EBIKWATA KU KICONCO
Ebisoomooza ku mitendera egy’enjawulo mu bufumbo n’engeri y’okubivvuunuka
Lwaki abasajja abamu tebaagala mbeerera?...
ABAMU basala magezi ga kubuvaamu, sso ng’oyo atannabufuna asiiba asaba n’okwegayirira Lugaba amufunire omutuufu.
Bano baabadde mu kifo ekisanyukirwamu mu Kampala gye buvuddeko nga beewaamu.
Ababulimu abamu babuwanda lulusu olw’okusoomoozebwa kwe babusanzeemu, ate ababunoonya beesunga ddi lwe babuyingira, olw’essanyu lye basuubira okubusangamu. Obwo bwe bufumbo.
Abakkiririza mu Kristo bakimanyi nti obufumbo bukulu, era bwawandiikibwa ne mu Bayibuli mu ndagaano empya n’enkadde.
Era y’ensonga lwaki wadde oluusi bubaamu ebirumira, obufumbo tebusuubirwa kuggwaawo. Ate era n’okusoomoozebwa okubulimu tekusuubirwa kuggwaawo.
Kino kitegeeza ki? Kitegeeza nti ggwe abulimu n’asuubira okubufuna oteekeddwa okwesiba n’onywera okusobola okubutambuza.
Oluusi ekiremesa abantu okuwangaalira mu bufumbo bwe butamanya kiki ekisuubirwa ku buli mutendera gw’obufumbo.
Rebecca Masagazi 40, ow’e Lungujja Kitunzi agamba nti wadde omusajja gw’abeera naye si wa mpeta, naye amulinamu abaana babiri.
Agamba nti; “Ebbanga lye maze ne baze nfunye okusoomoozebwa okw’enjawulo nga nkufunira mu mitendera, kyokka ne ηηuma okutuusa bwe yafuuka omuntu abeereka naye.
Bwe yanteeka mu maka ge, twatadika okubeera ffenna mu mukwano omusuffu era oluvannyuma lw’emyaka ebiri, nafuna olubuto lw’omwana waffe asooka.
Wano munnange we yasalirawo okuggyayo emize gye gyonna, omwali okuyomba, obutampuliriza, wabula olw’okuba nali mmwagala nasalawo mbigumire.
Ekyo bwe kyaggwa ate n’agattako okukola ebbuba nga buli gw’andaba naye amunjagaza.
Olumu ng’azira n’emmeere, n’annangira nga bwe mpeeruuse, ng’awaka takyayagala kubeerawo amaka n’agandekera naye nga nkanya kuguma n’okusaba n’obutalaalaasa bitukwatako.
Kyokka ng’obugumiikiriza bwe butuusa ku lyengedde, omwami yagenda adda yekka mu mbeera ze ηηenda okulaba eby’oku -yomba abivuddeko, azzeemu okunnyingiza mu pulaani ze anneebuuzaako, bwe tutyo nga tuddawo mpolampola n’okutuusa kati omwami n’atereera. Kati emyaka giweze 15 wabula ssinga saaguma nandinobedde ku etaano.
Micheal Dumba Ssaabafumbo w’essaza lye Kampala, ne Marion Nakagiri Sekiziyivu omuwandiisi w’ekitongole ekitwala ensonga z’a bafumbo bagamba nti abafumbo bwe bamaliriza okukuba ebirayiro mu bufumbo balowooza nti buwedde, so nga baba boolekedde okuyita mu mitendera gy’obufumbo egibaamu ebiwonvu n’ebikko.
Buli mutendera gubaamu okusoomoozebwa era abamu bakoma ku gusooka.
Guno gubaawo okuva abaagalana we basalirawo okubeera bonna nga guyinza okumala emyaka ebiri. Wano we wabeera omukwano ogw’ekimmemmette.
Essanyu liba lingi olw’ebingi ebisanyusa ebikolebwamu ng’okweyita amannya agawaana, okutambula wamu, okutona ebirabo ne kalonda omulala.
Kyokka wadde gulimu essanyu, abafumbo waliwo ebintu bye balina okusuubira okubasoomooza ku mutendera guno, omuli:
 Omusajja suubira ensaasaanya okweyongera kuba kati ababadde bakuyambako mu bintu ebimu omuwala gy’abadde abeera avuddeyo ggwe omuvunaanyizibwako.
Olina okutandika okukola emirimu egimu gy’obadde tokola. Okugeza, omuwala bw’oba obadde ojja ewa muganzi wo ng’osanga engoye ze ddobbi yazoozezza, manya nti ogenda kutandika okuzooza ate bwe muba emmere mubadde mulya ngule, manya nti okufumba kuyingiddewo.
Ku mutendera guno era w’olabira obuze munno bwe yakukweka, omuli okuyomba, obunafu, obucaafu n’obulala. Ku mutendera guno abamu we bakoma olw’obuze buno nga balaba tebajja kubusobola. Wabula era abagusukka bangi ne bagenda mu gw’okubiri.
OMUTENDERA OGW’OKUBIRI
Guno gwe mutendera ogw’okukula okuva mu bantu ababiri, era wano erinnya ly’amaka we litandikira okuvaayo. Mufuna abantu ababeegattako gamba ng’abaana be muzaala, abeηηanda, abakozi n’abalala. Guno guba mutendera gwa kutuukiriza bye mubadde mwesuubiza.
Mu mutendera guno, omukwano gw’ekimmemmette gutandika okusalika, essanyu ne likendeera olw’okusoomoozebwa okutera okubaawo omuli;
Omuwendo gw’abantu be mubeera nabo gweyongera. Okugeza abaana abazaaliddwa n’eηηanda babeegattako, ekitegeeza nti n’ensaasaanya yeeyongera.
 Abawi b’amagezi bajja mu mukwano gwammwe. Olumu abafumbo bwe bataba na ddiini, okweraguza kutandika, omusajja bamuwa amagezi oluusi agaba amakyamu, ate nga n’omukazi waliwo abamuwa amagezi agatali malungi.
Ku mutendera guno buli omu alwana okuba omutuufu.
Endabika ya munno gy’oli ekyuka, okugeza omusajja atandika okulaba omukyala ng’atamusaana olw’ebbeere eriba liyonkebwa omwana nga litandise okugwa era n’obwenzi emirundi egimu wano we buyingirira mu maka naddala ku ludda lw’abasajja.
Ekikolwa ky’okwerowoozako kitandika mu bufumbo, buli omu alaba alina okubaako ky’akola yekka.
Omukyala atandika okufuna endowooza y’okuzimba akayumba ke kuba alaba obufumbo buyuuga n’omwami atandika okwagala okugula ebibye.
Guno guba mutendera gwa kwenaazaako nnaku gye muyiseemu mu mutendera ogw’okubiri n’ogusooka era ebimu ku biyamba okuzza essanyu ebibeerawo mu mutendera guno bye bino wammanga.
Abaana be muzadde bakula ne bafuuka olujegere olubagatta n’obwetaavu bw’okubeera ewamu nabo.
Okuteesa kutandika okukomawo, buli omu n’amanya obuvunaanyizibwa bwe, olwo ne mutandika okuteeka ebigendererwa byammwe mu bikolwa.
 Mu mutendera guno mubamu ebintu eby’enjawulo ebikolebwa ng’okukuza ennaku ez’enjawulo okugeza Valentayini, amazaalibwa gammwe, wamu n’okweyungibwako emikwano emituufu era egyammwe mwembi.
Abaana abaazaalibwa ebbali wano we batera okujjira mu maka. Abaana bano batera kufunibwa mu mutendera ogwokubiri, omusajja w’alabira omukazi ng’atakyamusaana ne yeeyiiyaako ebbali gy’azaalira abaana.
Watera okubaawo enkyukakyuka mu byenfuna, okugeza omu ku bafumbo nga takyakola, akola n’atandika okumuyisaamu amaaso.
Obusobozi mu nkola y’ebintu bukyuka. Okugeza wano endwadde nga sukaali ne puleesa zizuuka ku munno oba mwembi era mwesanga ng’ensonga z’obuliri muyinza okuba nga temukyazeeyunira nnyo.
OMUTENDERA OGW’OKUNA
Guno gutwalibwa nti gwe mutendera ogusembayo oba guyite omutendera gw’okukulana. Guno gwogerwako ng’omutendera gw’ebirungi oba amakungula.
Mu mutendera guno abafumbo we baddirayo mu mukwano ogwasooka era nga buli omu ayagala okuba munne waali. Bwe muba mwazaala abaana, guba mutendera gwa kulabirirwa.
Okusoomooza okubaamu tekutera kuba kungi mu bafumbo mpozzi okufuna entondo n’oluusi okulwalalwala olw’omubiri okunafuwa.
ENGERI GYE MUYITA MU KUSOOMOOZEBWA
Marion Nakagiri agamba nti olw’okuba omuntu muba mwasalawo okubeera mwenna mu bulungi ne mu bubi, bwe wabaawo okuyuuga mu bufumbo mulina kulwana okusigala awamu nga muteekawo obugumiikiriza era buli omu n’alwana okutuukiriza obuvunaanyizibwa bwe n’okugolola ensobi nga muli wamu kuba obufumbo kitegeeza kubeera bumu mu byonna bye mukola.
Nakagiri era agamba nti buli bufumbo nga tebunnatandika wabaawo abakulu ababeera balambika abafumbo okugeza bwe buba bwa ddiini, abafumbo balina okujjukira ebyo bye baalagaana n’ebyababuulirirwa ne babyekwatako okuyita mu muyaga gwonna.

1 Wajja kubaawo olutuula olw'enjawulo ku ssaawa 8:00 ez'olweggulo ababaka lwe bajja kuyisa ekiteese ekisemba emirimu gya Aronda abadde Minisita ow'ensonga Ezomunda nga yaliko n'omuduumizi w'amagye wakati wa 2003 ne 2013.
2 Ekiteeso ekisiima emirimu gya Aronda kya kwanjibwa Katikkiro wa Uganda, Dr. Ruhakana Rugunda kisembebwe akulira oludda oluvuganya mu Palamenti Wafula Oguttu n'oluvannyuma ababaka ab'enjawulo nabo bamwogereko okutuuka ku ssaawa 10:00 ez'olweggulo.
3 Ng'ekiteeso tekinnayanjibwa, wajja kubaawo okuganzika ebimuli ku ssanduuko y'omugenzi nga Sipiika wa Palamenti Rebecca Kadaga, y'ajja okusooka, addirirwe, omumyuka wa Pulezidenti Edward Sekandi, kuddeko omumyuka wa Sipiika Jacob Oulanyah, Katikkiro Rugunda, addeko n'abalala bagoberere
Amaka ga kitaawe wa mukyala wa Aronda agasangibwa ku kyalo Nyaburondo Nyakagyeme Rukungiri
4 Omulambo gwakuggyibwa mu Palamenti ku ssaawa 10:15 gutwalibwe mu maka g'omugenzi e Buziga gye gunaaggyibwa ku Lwokutaano okutwalibwe e Kololo mu kusaba okw'eddiini zonna.
5 Gen. Aronda waakuziikibwa ku kyalo Nyakiju mu disitulikiti y'e Rukungiri ku Ssande nga September 20, 2015 ng'emikolo gyakutandika n'okusaba ku ssaawa 3:00 ez'oku makya.

Okwagala kwe kuba n’omutima omugabi, y’engeri gye tulabamu abalala wamu n’okufaayo ku byetaago byabwe.
Okuggyako nga tulina okwagala, ne bwe tuyitimuka tutya, kiba tekirina mugaso.
Okwagala kuva mu kusaasira kwe tulina eri abo ababonaabona, wadde nga abamu tuba tetubamanyi.
Okwagala kutusisimula ne tufaayo ku byetaago byabwe, nga tubawa obuyambi wamu n’ekisa.
Tuleetawo enkyukankyuka eya nnamaddala mu bulamu bwabwe.
Bwe tubeera n’okwagala, tetusalira balala musango.
Okwagala kwe kwolesa okufaayo.
Okwagala kutondawo ekibiina ky’abaagalana.
“Era kaakano tubeere mu kukkiriza, okusuubira wamu n’okwagala, byonsatule; wabula ekisingira ddala kwebyo byonna kwe kwagala.”
Empisa y’Okwagala.
Ngabana n’abalala ku ebyo byennina.
Nzikkiriza nti n'abalala bakola ensobi.
Neebaza olw’ekirabo ky’Okwagala. Kifuula obulamu bwange okuba obw’omukisa.

Envuza z’e Busoga kikolimo kya Katonda - Judith Babirye
Judith Babirye eyasabye Abasoga okudda eri Katonda.
OMUYIMBI Judith Babirye agambye nti envunza ezitawaanya Abasoga kikolimo okuva ewa Katonda. Bwatyo asabye Abasoga okudda eri Omutonzi beenenye ebibi byonna bye bazze bakola omuli okutulugunya n’okutta basajja ba Katonda abaali bagenzeeyo okubalyowa emyoyo.
Bino yabyogedde wiiki ewedde mu lukung’aana lw’enjiri lwe yakubyeyo ne Bapaasita abalala.
“Mmwe Abasoga mwatta Bishop Hanington, e Mayuge, ab’e Jinja mwatulugunya n’okugoba Reinhard Bonnke mu 1990, kye ndowooza nti kyanyiiza Katonda era by’ebyo ebikolimo bye mulozaako kati.
Babirye yabasabye okuddamu okwegayirira Mukama Katonda abasonyiwe ebibi bye baakola bwe baba baagala okufuna emirembe n’okuwona endwadde zino.

Bukedde Online - KCCA eyimirizza okumenya amayumba g'abatuuze mu Kampala
Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala
Ebifaananyi
BUKEDDE W’OLWOMUKAAGA YATUUSE DDA MU KATALE NG’ALIMU BINO
EBIKWATA KU KICONCO
KCCA eyimirizza okumenya amayumba g'abatuuze mu Kampala
Ebiri mu lipoota y'okutta Andrew Felix Kaweesi...
OLUKIIKO lwa KCCA luyimirizza okumenya amayumba g’abatuuze mu bitundu eby’enjawulo mu Kampala nti basooke bakole okunoonyereza okw’enjawulo ku bigendererwa ebikirimu.
Lukwago ng'akubiriza olukiiko lwa KCCA
Ekitongole ekitegekera ekibuga kyafulumya olukalala lw’amayumba agasoba mu 500 agagenda okumenyebwa olw’obutaba ku pulaani era bannannyini go ne balumiriza nti waliwo abagagga mu Kampala nti be bali emabega w’olukwe luno .
Eggulo Loodi Meeya Erias Lukwago yatadde akulira ekitongole ekitegekera ekibuga Moses Atwine, ku nninga annyonnyole oba olukalala lutuufu n’akakasa nti lutuufu era bakansala ne bayisa ekiteeso obutageza kukwata ku nnyumba ya muntu yenna.
Baagambye nti balina okusooka okuddayo okwetegereza balabe oba nga ddala ennyumba zino zisaanye okumenyebwa ye bwe babeera baakuzimenya balina kuyita mu mitendera ki okusobola okulaba nga bannanyini zo ate tebanyigiriziddwa n’okuweebwayo ekiseera ekimala okutegulawo ebintu byabwe ate n’abalina obusobozi bazikyusa zituukane n’omutindo gw’ekibuga.
Mu lukiiko luno olwatudde ku City Hall eggulo, Lukwago yagobezza bakansala lipooti erimu bye baasalawo mu lukiiko lw’okutabagana ye lwe yagaana okwetabamu n’alusindikamu looya we Nathan Mpenja kyokka ne bamugoba.
Mu bimu ku byamutabudde, yategeezezza nti lipooti gye baabadde bamwanjulira teraga ani akulira ekitongole kya KCCA kubanga enfunda nnyingi Jennifer Musisi yeeyanjula nga y’akikulira ye Lukwago ky’awakanya.
Lukwago yanokoddeyo akatundu akalaga nti, minisita wa Kampala ne bakansala be bamulinako obuyinza kyokka nga mu bye baasalawo tewali we bamwogererako nti y’akulira ekitongole kya KCCA era Jennifer Musisi ali wansi we.
EKIFO KYA BASALIRWABAKIRONDEDDEMU OMULALA
Lukwago yabasabye lipooti ekwata ku ssente ezibadde ziva mu kivvulu kya kanivo (Kampala Carnival) ekyayimiriziddwa kyokka ne bamugamba nti nayo
yabadde tennaba kuggwa era n’alagira nti zonna bateekwa okuzimuwa mu bbanga lya mwezi gumu.
Oluvannyuma Lukwago yayanjulidde bakansala ekifo ky’okugereka obusuulu bw’ebizimbe ekibaddemu omubaka wa Munisipaali y’e Bugiri, Asuman Basaalirwa n’ategeeza nti kikalu era yakirondeddemu munna FDC Dan Wandera Ogalo era awatali kwesalamu ne bamusemba akitwale.
Bakansala nabo baakyusizza obukiiko ne balonda obupya era kansala Ismail Ddamba yasigadde ku by’enjigiriza, Bruhan Byaruhanga wa by’amateeka, Abubaker Kawalya, kutegekera kibuga, ate Kenedy Okello kikula ky’abantu ne Alice Omony byabulamu.

Obuli bw’enguzi: Minisita Beti Kamya alabudde abakozesa erinnya lye _ NTV
Home » News » Akawungeezi » Obuli bw’enguzi: Minisita Beti Kamya alabudde abakozesa erinnya lye
Obuli bw’enguzi: Minisita Beti Kamya alabudde abakozesa erinnya lye
Minisita wa Kampala Beti Kamya alabudde abo abakozesa erinya lye nga basaba abantu sente bakikomye kubanga anakwatibwako kakumujjuutuka.
Minisita era ategeezezza nga bwewaliwo owa poliisi akolera ku poliisi ya USAFI anoonyerezebwako ku bigambibwa nti aliko ensimbi zeyasaba abasuubuzi okusobola okubakolera ku nsonga zabwe , nga eddoozi lyona lyakwatibwa ku katambi era nerisindikibwa ewa ssenkulu wa poliisi.
Minisita Kamya olwaleero asisinkanye abagoba ba takisi okuva mu ppaaka ye Nateete abamusanze mu ofiisi ye enkya yaleero era nalagira poliisi okutuukiriza ebiragiro byeyawa ku ppaaka z’amotoka .

4. Hwa Yobi - Bye Bye Bye

Ya mukulu w'abayimbi; Zabbuli ya batabani ba Koola; ery'ekyalamoosi. Oluyimba.
1 Katonda kye kiddukiro n'amaanyigaffe, Omubeezi ddala atabula mu kulaba ennaku.
2 Kyetunaavanga tulema okutya, ensi ne bw'eneekyukanga, N'ensozi ne bwe zinaasigukanga mu buziba obw'ennyanja;
3 Amazzi gaayo ne bwe ganaayiranga ne bwe ganeekuluumululanga, N'ensozi ne bwe zinaakankananga n'okwetabula kwayo. (Seera
4 Waliwo omugga, emyala gyagwo gisanyusa ekibuga kya Katonda, Ekifo ekitukuvu eky'eweema z'oyo ali waggulu ennyo.

1 Mukale mukube mu ngalo, mmwe amawanga gonna; Mwogerere waggulu eri Katonda n'eddoboozi ery'okuwangula.

3 Katonda waffe alijja, so talisirika; Omuliro gulirya mu maaso ge, Omuyaga omungi gulimwetooloola.

5 Mukugriaanye abatukuvu bange awamu we ndi; Abaalagaana nange endagaano n'essaddaaka.

10 Kubanga buli nsolo ey'omu kibira yange, N'ente ez'oku nsozi lukumi.
11 Ennyonyi zonna ez'oku nsozi nzimanyi: N'ensolo ez'omu nsiko zange.
12 Singa nnumwa enjala, sandikubuulidde: Kubanga ensi yange, n'okujjula kwayo.
13 Nze naalyanga ennyama eya seddume, Oba naanywanga omusaayi gw'embuzi?
14 Owenga Katonda ssaddaaka ey'okwebaza; Osasulenga obweyamo bwo ali waggulu ennyo:
15 Era onkoowoolenga ku lunaku olw'okulaba ennaku; Ndikuwonya, naawe olingulumiza nze!
16 Naye omubi Katonda amugamba nti Ofaayo ki okubuulira amateeka Bange, N'oyingiza endagaano yange mu kamwa ko?
17 Kubanga okyawa okuyigirizibwa, N'ebigambo byange obisuula ennyuma wo.
18 Bwe walaba omubbi walagaana naye, Era wassa ekimu n'abenzi.

23 Buli ampa ssaddaaka ey'okwebaza angulumiza; Naye alongoosa obulungi ekkubo lye Ndimulaga obulokozi bwa Katonda.

2 Onsaasire, ai Katonda, mu kisa kyo; Mu bungi obw'okusaasira kwo sangula ebyonoono byange byonna.
3 Onnaalize ddala mu bubi bwange, Omiongoose mu kwonoona kwange.
4 Ggwe, ggwe wekka, ggwe nnayonoona. Ne nkola ekibi mu maaso go: Obeere omutuukirivu bw'oyogera, Osinge omusango bw'osala.
5 Laba, nze natondebwa mu bubi; Ne mu kwonoona mmange mwe yanzaalira.
6 Laba, ggwe oyagala eby'omunda eby'amazima; Era mu mwoyo ogutalabika onommanyisa amagezi.
7 Ontukuze n'ezoobu, nange naaba mulungi: Onnaaze, nange naaba mutukuvu okusinga omuzira.
8 Ompulize essanyu a'okwesiima; Amagumba ge wamenya gasanyuke.

1 Ondokole mu balabe bange, ai Katonda wange: Ongulumize eri abo abangolokokerako.

Ya mukulu w'ebayimbi; ey'ekiauseniedusi: Mikutamu ya Dewudi, ey'okuyiguiza: bwe yewakana ne Alamu-nakalayimu ne Alamu-zoba, Yowaabu n'akomawo, n'atta ku Edomu kakumi mu enkumi bbiri mu Kiwonvu eky'omunnyo.

12 Katonda ye anaatukozanga eby'obuzira: Kubanga oyo ye anaalinnyiriranga ku balabe baffe.

JW Olukuŋŋaana lw’Obulamu bw’Ekikristaayo Maaki 6-12, 2017
“Ndi Wamu Naawe Okukulokola”: (Ddak. 10)
[Mulabe vidiyo, Ennyanjula y’Ekitabo kya Yeremiya.]
Yer 1:6—Yeremiya yali awulira nga tasobola kukola mulimu ogwali gumuweereddwa (w11 3/15 29 ¶4)
Okusoma Bayibuli: (Ddak. 4 oba obutawera) Yer 4:1-10
Tegeka Ennyanjula z’Omwezi Guno: (Ddak. 15) Mulabe vidiyo eziraga ennyanjula ze tuyinza okukozesa, era oluvannyuma lwa buli vidiyo, mukubaganye ebirowoozo.
Ebikolebwa Ekibiina: (Ddak. 7) Mulabe vidiyo erina omutwe Ebikolebwa Ekibiina, eya Maaki.
Kaweefube w’Okuyita Abantu ku Kijjukizo Ajja Kutandika nga Maaki 18: (Ddak. 8) Okwogera okw’okuweebwa omulabirizi w’obuweereza, nga kwesigamiziddwa ku katabo Obulamu bw’Ekikristaayo n’Obuweereza Bwaffe aka Febwali 2016, olupapula 8. Buli omu muwe akapapula akayita abantu ku Kijjukizo, oluvannyuma oyogere ku ebyo ebikalimu. Bategeeze enteekateeka ey’okubugaba mu kitundu kye mubuuliramu.
Obulamu bw’Ekikristaayo n’Obuweereza Bwaffe Enteekateeka y’Enkuŋŋaana Eza Maaki 6-12, 2017

BUKEDDE W’OLWOMUKAAGA YATUUSE DDA MU KATALE NG’ALIMU GANO
Bakunyizza omujaasi ku by’ettaka ly’e Mubende...
Kadaga yajjukizza ababaka nti balina okwebuuza ku bantu kubanga ennyingo esooka eya Konsitityusoni egamba nti obuyinza buli mu bantu era be balina okusalawo engeri n’enkola gye balina okukulemberwa.
Raphael Magyezi (Igara West) ng’ayanjulira Palamenti ebbago ly’etteeka okukyusa mu Konsitityusoni erya 2017.
OMUBAKA Raphael Magyezi abuuse omuziziko ogwokubiri mu kaweefube we ow’okukyusa mu Konsityusoni okuggyawo ekkomo ery’emyaka 75 Pulezidenti kw’akoma okwesimbawo bw’ayanjizza ebbago ly’etteeka mu lutuula lwa Palamenti olubadde olw’ekimpoowooze.
Magyezi akiikirira Igara West kimutwalidde eddakiika emu n’ekitundu okwanja ebbago ly’etteeka, ‘THE CONSTITUTIONAL (AMMENDEMENT) (NO2) 2017 mu lutuula olutajjumbiddwa nga lwetabiddwaamu ababaka 120 bokka mu babaka 449.
Okwawukanako nga bwe gwali ku Lwokubiri n’Olwokusatu lwa wiiki ewedde Palamenti bwe yajjjula n’ebooga ng’ababaka naddala ab’oludda oluvuganya beesomye okulemesa ekiteeso kya Magyezi okuweebwa olukusa okuleeta ekiteeso kye, ku mulundi guno Palamenti tebaddeemu bbugumu kubanga ab’oludda oluvuganya baazize olutuula ate bannaabwe abalala 24 bakyali ku kibonerezo ekyabaweebwa Sipiika olw’okusiwuuka empisa mu lutuula olw’oku Lwokubiri oluwedde.
Essaawa zaabadde 9:34 ez’olweggulo, Magyezi n’ayanja ebbago n’asembebwa baminisita n’abamu ku babaka ba Palamentia aba NRM abaayimiridde ng’akabonero akalaga nti bamusembye okwanja ebbago ly’etteeka okukyusa mu Konsitityusoni
Nga bw’eri enkola ya Palamenti ng’ebbago ly’etteeka lyanjulwa omulundi ogusooka, n’ebbago lya Magyezi terikubaganyiziddwaako birowoozo, Sipiika Rebecca Kadaga n’alisindika mu kakiiko k’ebyamateeka akakulirwa omubaka Jacob Marksons Oboth akiikirira West Budama South okwekenneenyezebwa.
Akakiiko k’ebyamateeka mu Palamenti katuulako ababaka 23 nga kuliko aba NRM 13, abatalina kibiina bali mukaaga okuli ne ssentebe Oboth-Oboth ate DP ne FDC balina ababaka babiri babiri. Katikkiro wa Uganda Dr. Ruhakana Rugunda yategeezezza nti kirungi nti ebbago lyanjuddwa n’agamba nti buli Munnayuganda alina omukisa okuwa ekirowoozo kye ku bbago lino.
Yagambye nti abantu abalina endowooza enz’enjawulo ku bbago lino basobola okuyita mu babaka baabwe oba okuleeta ebirowoozo byabwe mu kakiiko bijja kuwulirwa.
Omubaka Abasi Agaba (Kitagwenda) nga y’omu ku batuula ku kakiiko k’ebyamateeka yagambye nti akakiiko kagenda kutuula kayite abantu okuwa ebirowoozo byabwe ku bbago lino; Omubaka Ismail Alli Ogama (Lower Madi) yagambye nti agenda kwebuuza ku balonzi be era ebirowoozo bye banaamuwa ajja okubituusa mu Palamenti nga takyusizzaamu.
Ebbago lya Magyezi lirimu ennyingo nnya okuli eky’okuggyawo ekkomo ku myaka 75 omuntu yenna ayagala okwesimbawo ku Bwapulezidenti gy’alina okubeera nagyo.
Akawaayiro ak’emyaka 35 omuntu ayagala okubeera Pulezidenti oba ssentebe wa LC5 gy’alina okuba ng’awezezza nako kagenda kukyusibwa gidde ku myaka 18.
Ebbago era lirimu ennyingo okukyusa mu Konsitityusoni okwongera ebbanga ku nnaku 10 omuntu atamatidde na bivudde mu kulonda kw’alina okutwala omusango mu kkooti n’okwongera kkooti ey’oku ntikko obudde bw’okuwulira omusango gw’ebyokulonda.

Omuko Ogusooka
Eno Ye Kampala

Paasita Muto ayogedde ku bituuse ku Bobi...
Ebikumi n’ebikumi by’Abasiraamu okuva mu kibuga ky’e Mukono n’ebitundu ebirinanyeewo beeyiye mu bungi ku muzikiti omukulu mu kibuga ky’e Mukono okusaala Iddi y’okusala ebisolo ku Lwokubiri.
Abasiraamu nga basala ente e Mukono oluvannyuma lw'okusaala. Ente ttaano ze zaasaliddwa ku muzikiti guno. Ebifaananyi bya HENRY NSUBUGA
Abasiraamu nga basala ente e Mukono oluvannyuma lw'okusaala. Ente ttaano ze zaasaliddwa ku muzikiti guno.
Sheikh Kakumba mu kwogera ku kwekalakaasa okusasaanidde ebitundu by’eggwanga eby’enjawulo, ng’ab’ekalakaasi balaga obutali bumativu olw’ababaka ba palamenti okuli ne Robert Kyagulanyi abaakwatibwa n’okutulugunyizibwa mu ngeri ez’enjawulo oluvannyuma lw’okunoonya obululu bw’okujjuza ekifo ky’omubaka wa Arua munisipaali yalabudde abavubuka okwewala okwenyigira obutereevu mu kwekalakaasa okwo.
Abaana nga beetegereza nga babaaga ente ezimu ku ttaano ezaasaliddwa ku muzikiti e Mukono.
Yagambye nti kikyamu abavubuka okukozesebwanga mu mbeera nga zino ez’okwekalakaasa ng’ebiseera bingi obulamu bwabwe butokomokeddemu olwo ne baleka abantu baabwe omuli abaana, abakyala n’enganda zaabwe nga babonaabona.
Ssentebe w’omuzikiti guno, Ffeffekka Sserubogo naye yavumiridde ekya gavumeti okukozesa eryanyi ku beekalakaasi n’asaba ensonga ezibaviirako okwekalakaasa ze ziba zikolebwako okusinga okujjayo abasirikale mu buli bbaalakisi naye ng’ensonga eyekalakaasisa abantu tegonjoddwa.
